## System: B — Dashboard V2 harness wiring, producers, knowledge/runtime surfaces, and B-plan delivery

Family B is the dashboard and core harness wiring track: V2 Tauri 2.10 / Svelte 5 / Vite 5 / three.js orbit shell, SSB producers that activate real UI, knowledge/reasoning/orchestration producers, Track D command wiring, autonomous mode, multi-runtime backends, framing/audit/Cosmos, and the B-0..B-43 session contracts.

Status rule: deep ledgers and later in-file closures beat the 2026-05-17 summary snapshot. Explicit SUPERSEDED markers and higher plan numbers win target state. Losing claims appear as **Superseded:** bullets with SUP/atom anchors.

Source (read-only): `docs/thefinalglance/feature-discovery/aggregates/B-AGGREGATE.md`.

### B-1: Path unification (.elai -> .elai_cc)
- **Status:** shipped
- **Anchors:** `B1-001` @ DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:33; `B1-002` @ DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:34; `B1-003` @ DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:35; `B1-004` @ DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:36; `B1-005` @ DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:37; `B1-006` @ DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:38; `B1-007` @ DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:39; `B1-008` @ DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:40; `B1-009` @ DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:41; `B1-010` @ DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:42
- **Composes with:**
  - `B-002/X-001` ↔ B-2, B-31, B-4: B-1 is the explicit prerequisite for both B-4 model/vector placement and B-31 checkpoint metadata placement; it also declares B-2 parallel-safe (B-1 lines 6-8; B-4 lines 5-8; B-31 lines 16-19).
  - `B-002/X-002` ↔ B-31, B-4: B-1’s canonical helper surface is the shared authority for CLI/orchestrator/dashboard reachability; B-4 and B-31 must consume the same <code>.elai_cc</code> root rather than introducing feature-local literals (B-1 lines 58-69, 110-127).
  - `B-002/X-005` ↔ B-4: B-4’s model staging explicitly relies on B-1’s <code>models_dir_path</code>; B-1 independently bans old <code>.elai/models/</code> literals (B-1 lines 87-89; B-4 lines 80-89).
  - `B-002/X-006` ↔ B-31: B-31’s idempotent <code>migrateAbsolutePathKeys</code> is explicitly a post-B-1 compatibility migration, tying resume identity to relative canonical paths (B-31 lines 161-165).
  - `B-002/X-011` ↔ B-31: Once B-1 and B-31 compose, checkpoint/file-hash/lock state and the DKG/BM25/vector data they protect resolve beneath one canonical project root; neither source authorizes a second metadata root.
  - `B-002/X-017` ↔ B-2, B-24, B-31, B-34, B-39, B-4: Determinism forms a shared boundary: B-1 centralizes paths; B-2 and B-4 use injected clocks; B-24 bans GPU SSIM/LPIPS and selects mode deterministically; B-31 uses blake3/relative IDs/RAII; B-34 uses BTree collections/case-exact rules; B-39
  - `B-002/X-019` ↔ B-24, B-31, B-39: Four activation gates coexist without conflict: project initialization (B-1), media-step presence (B-24), indexing-disable environment flag (B-31), and per-session <code>/reason</code> toggle default-off (B-39).
  - `B-005/X06` ↔ B-29, B-31, B-32, B-33, B-34, B-35: : B-29 requires B-1’s canonical DKG path, integrates B-31 watcher invalidation, backs B-33 symbol visualization, shares cache payloads with B-34, and is parallel-safe with B-32/B-35 (<code>F06:1-10, 206-229, 301-302, 429-448</code>).
  - `B-005/X07` ↔ B-31, B-32, B-35: : B-32 requires B-1, is a natural B-31 batch-refactor sibling, adds minified telemetry to B-31’s progress panel, and supplies B-35’s artifact chunking (<code>F07:1-10, 205-228, 319-337, 373-379</code>).
  - `B1-035` → B-2, B-3: B-2 may run in parallel; B-3 depends on B-1 so real readers can replace stubs (lines 214-218).
- **Superseded:** `SUP-011` from `B1-009`: Canonical live consumers are <code>crates/cli/src/runtime_builder.rs:224</code>, <code>crates/cli/src/repl_command.rs:625</code>, and <code>crates/cli/src/live_cli.rs:3255</code>, each using <code>dkg_db_path</code>; the latter paths supersede older monolithic <code>main.rs</code> anchors (lines 74-79; 110-116; 185-187).
- `B1-001`: Objective: eliminate the split database path that leaves PageRank, CatRAG, A-RAG, Telescope/blast-radius consumers, and <code>DkgQuerierAdapter</code> empty even though the DKG is populated; estimated one 2–4 hour session, no prerequisite, highest-leverage B-plan fix (lines 1-8). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:33)_

- `B4-011`: <code>SsbDegradationNotifier</code> is constructed by <code>spawn_cascade_evaluator_loop</code>, has its dead-code allowance removed, and writes <code>knowledge_degradation</code> rows on mode transitions (lines 97-100). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:119)_

- `B4-015`: Circuit-breaker command returns per-model <code>state ∈ {Closed, HalfOpen, Open}</code>, <code>last_failure</code>, <code>last_success</code>, and <code>attempts</code>; drawer presents a five-row grid (lines 116-119). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:123)_

- `B10-011`: A contract must prove TaskRunner and REPL yield identical ContextBudget for equivalent input so neither path can silently drift back to `None`. (B10:L85-88) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:158)_

(B10:L90-102) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:159)_

- `B10-014`: Forbidden production states are `dispatch_context_profile: None`, absent `file_overlap_score`, and direct `ContextBudget::compute(raw_max, [0,0,0], 4096)`. (B10:L109-113) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:161)_
- `B10-015`: Observable acceptance runs `context_budget_smoke_ssb`, expects `context_budget_computed`, and requires a visible nonzero per-slot budget display. SQL evidence must show eight nonzero allocations in the latest row. (B10:L114-135) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:162)_
- `B10-016`: Acceptance also protects the existing 3E “100-config zero-sum invariant,” in addition to profile detection, all five replacements, overlap sorting, reduction events, exact weights, and parity. (B10:L137-146) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:163)_

The original does not independently prove model detection, all five sites, every reduction emission, parity, or the zero-sum regression gate; treat those as required behavior whose detailed completion evidence is absent in this chunk. (B10:L137-157; B0:L108, L152) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:165)_

  - `B-002/X-009` ↔ B-12, B-22, B-24, B-3, B-7: B-12 depends on B-3 registration, B-7 decomposition, and B-11 tokens; B-24 depends on B-7 media-bearing PipelineSpec and B-22 autonomous iteration; none of those prerequisites is silently supplied by this chunk (B-12 lines 7-9; B-24 lines 8

- `B11-001`: Objective is exact visual identity, but V2 rebaselines the work to a 1-2 hour verification/drift-backfill because most was already shipped. It is pure frontend, has no prerequisite, and is parallel-safe with B-12..B-20. (B11:L1-10) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:171)_

(B11:L25-49) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:173)_
- `B11-004`: Active paths are `crates/dashboard-v2/src/styles/tokens.css`, `index.html`, and `src/lib/`; legacy V1 paths must never be edited. (B11:L42-49) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:174)_
- `B11-005`: Exact background tokens: `bg-void #0a0e12`, `bg-base #0f1318`, `bg-surface #151a20`, `bg-hover #1a2028`, `bg-input #1e2530`. (B11:L72-81) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:175)_
- `B11-006`: Exact brand tokens: teal `#1B7A6E`, teal-soft `#1B7A6E40`, teal-ghost `#1B7A6E15`, sparing teal-bright `#24a08e`; purple `#7C5CBF`, purple-soft `#7C5CBF30`, purple-ghost `#7C5CBF12`. (B11:L82-88) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:176)_
- `B11-007`: Exact semantic/text tokens: text-primary `#d0d7de` (not white), secondary `#6e7681`, ghost `#383e47`, pass reuses `#1B7A6E`, warn `#c49b2a`, fail `#c44040`, info `#5088c0`. (B11:L89-95) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:177)_
- `B11-008`: Typography: primary stack `"JetBrains Mono", "Fira Code", monospace`; display `"Segoe UI"` weight 200 only; `xs=10px` with `0.05em`, `sm=12`, `base=13`, `lg=15`, `xl=18` with `0.15em`; line height 1.6 text and 1.4 code. (B11:L97-103) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:178)_
- `B11-009`: Layout: 1px `bg-hover` borders with teal only active/focused; 6px panel/card and 3px control corners; no shadows; 8px base spacing, 12-16px panel padding and 8px inline padding. (B11:L104-109) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:179)_
- `B11-010`: General transitions are opacity 120ms ease and transform 150ms ease. Scrollbars are 4px, `bg-hover` track, teal-soft thumb, hidden until hover. (B11:L109-110) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:180)_
- `B11-011`: Icons are thin-stroke Lucide: 16px standard, 20px sidebar, monochrome secondary text, teal only when active. (B11:L111-112) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:181)_
- `B11-012`: Mechanical scale correction map is 11 -> 10, 13 -> 12, 14 -> 13, 16 -> 15, and 20 -> 18 pixels. Any meaning that cannot map cleanly to the palette requires a user decision rather than invented color. (B11:L114-126) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:182)_
- `B11-013`: Animation details: thinking is a 2px teal line pulsing opacity 20% -> 50% -> 20% over 2s; completion is a 100ms teal-ghost flash; failure is a 2px fail-color left border fading in 200ms. (B11:L128-132) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:183)_
- `B11-014`: Panel switch is 80ms fade-out then 120ms fade-in; active progress dot scales 100% -> 110% -> 100% over 3s; bouncing, shake, and decorative animation are forbidden. (B11:L132-134) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:184)_
- `B11-015`: Orbit-specific audit values are `constellation-breathe` 11s, ambient flow on inactive edges, `dashflow` on active branch, and idle shooting star about every 14s. (B11:L34-36) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:185)_
- `B11-016`: WIRING_CONTRACT outputs updated CSS variables, JetBrains Mono `@font-face`, and Lucide imports; all Svelte components consume tokens; the root mount makes the change reachable; visual snapshots, not SSB, are the observable evidence. (B11:L140-159) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:186)_

### B-14: Live token-streaming chat
- **Status:** shipped
- **Anchors:** `B14-E01` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:69; `B14-E02` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:70; `B14-E03` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:71; `B14-E04` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:72; `B14-E05` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:73; `B14-E06` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:74; `B14-E07` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:75; `B14-E08` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:76; `B14-E09` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:77; `B14-E10` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:78
- **Composes with:**
  - `B-002/X-010` ↔ B-39, B-5, B-8: B-39 depends on B-5 role configuration, B-8 PipelineGrc production wiring, and B-14 streaming; its passthrough fallback deliberately preserves B-14 behavior when disabled/skipped/failing (B-39 lines 8-12, 245-278).
  - `B-004/X-E02` ↔ B-36: B-14 and B-36 D.7 explicitly share Tauri emit streaming. B-14 requires consistent `turn`, `streaming_started`, `response`, `token`, `stop`; B-36’s one-shot command emits the first three (B-14 L42-L53; B-36 L158-L162, L226-L234).
  - `B-004/X-E03` ↔ B-5: B-14 permits its InterventionCard to reuse B-5’s CsqBlockCard/intervention work; both surface safety-relevant user decisions in Command/Reasoning UI (B-14 L167-L174; B-5 L99-L103, L164-L170).
  - `B-004/X-I01` ↔ B-36: A unified V2 chat path likely uses B-36 AppState/subprocess/event plumbing to host B-14’s `stream_chat` and cancellation handle. Both halves are explicit, but neither source states the final shared type/module boundary.
  - `B-004/X-I08` ↔ B-36: B-14’s “no thinking messages” and B-36’s live `web_research_step` imply progress should flow through Activity/orbit while Command displays milestones/tokens/interventions; this composed routing is consistent but not specified as one end-to-
  - `B-004/X-A02` ↔ B-36: B-14 Stop needs an AppState cancellation handle; B-36 AppState lists only SSB writer, ResearchCache, and ProjectPaths. The cancellation registry’s owner, lifetime, and concurrency semantics are missing.
- **Superseded:** `SUP-050` from `B14-E17`: Original touch set names commands, ChatWindow, SlashPalette, InterventionCard (possibly reuse B-5), new intent classifier, and `workflow/reasoning/B-14-decisions.md`; V2 rebaseline supersedes V1 frontend paths.
- **Superseded:** `SUP-065` from `SD-B14-04`: Correction: source’s deterministic/files sections retain obsolete V1 paths despite the V2 rebaseline (L27-L64, L142-L175); checks should target V2.
- `B14-E01`: Dashboard chat must stream real model tokens from sidecar `/v1/messages` SSE instead of only writing an intent row. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:69)_
- `B14-E02`: V2 replaces ChatWindow with `CommandWindow.svelte` plus `CommandPalette.svelte`: a vertical card list, outlined `.input-shell` using `--bg-input` and focused `--teal-line`, and a slash dialog anchored above the shell that auto-opens when `/` starts the textarea. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:70)_
- `B14-E03`: Palette groups are Workflow (`/task /fix /research /continue`), Project (`/explore /status /config`), and Help (`/ask`). Buttons use outlined language only: teal Approve; neutral Reject/Modify/Redirect; solid-fill CTAs are prohibited. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:71)_
- `B14-E04`: `submit_chat_message` maps to new V2 `stream_chat`; ChatWindow maps to CommandWindow; SlashPalette maps to existing CommandPalette; InterventionCard is either a new V2 component or a CommandWindow card variant; `intent_classifier.rs` is unchanged and V2-agnostic. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:72)_
- `B14-E05`: `stream_chat(message)` uses Tauri 2 `Window::emit("token", ...)`; B-36 D.7 shares the SSE-via-emit pattern. Channel vocabulary must stay consistent: `turn`, `streaming_started`, `response`, `token`, `stop`. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:73)_
- `B14-E06`: CommandWindow imports `listen` from `@tauri-apps/api/event`; Stop invokes `cancel_stream` with a server-side AppState cancellation handle. Token buffer uses Svelte 5 `$state`; `$effect` owns subscription cleanup; legacy `$:` and `createEventDispatcher` are banned. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:74)_
- `B14-E07`: The reused path is `ConversationRuntime -> RuntimeClient(Local) -> sidecar /v1/messages -> DoLaSteeringProcessor -> model.generate -> SSE -> render`, already wired in CLI/REPL but absent from dashboard. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:75)_
- `B14-E08`: New `stream_chat(message)` either returns token events as a stream or emits them through Tauri, reusing the CLI runtime; frontend renders tokens incrementally with a typing indicator while streaming. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:76)_
- `B14-E09`: Every input is classified before routing as `TASK|FIX|EXPLORE|CONFIG|INTERVENE|STEER|ABORT`; slash commands/keywords are deterministic and ambiguous natural language falls back to the LLM, with a FLOW-4 disambiguation card. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:77)_
- `B14-E10`: Slash autocomplete filters `/task /fix /explore /config /status /continue`; response types include Status, Intervention, Error, Disambiguation, and Steering acknowledgment using the specification layouts. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:78)_
- `B14-E11`: Placeholder text is stateful: IDLE = “describe a task, ask a question, or type / for commands”; EXECUTING = “add constraints, or wait for intervention cards”; active intervention = “respond to the card above, or type to add context.” _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:79)_
- `B14-E12`: Command must not emit “thinking...” messages; work progress belongs in ACTIVITY, and COMMAND updates only for input needs or milestones. A Stop icon appears during streaming and cancels the in-flight sidecar request. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:80)_
- `B14-E13`: Produced symbols are `dashboard-v2/src-tauri/src/commands.rs::stream_chat` and `intent_classifier.rs::classify_intent`; consumers are CommandWindow/CommandPalette; reachability is `CommandWindow.svelte::submitInput`; observable evidence is `chat_message` plus character-by-character token rendering. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:81)_
- `B14-E14`: Remove the intent-only `submit_chat_message` stub and render tokens as they arrive, never only after completion. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:82)_
- `B14-E15`: Deterministic checks require `stream_chat` presence, all seven intent categories, and a `/task hello` smoke showing planning state. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:83)_
- `B14-E16`: Done requires real streaming, correct seven-way classification, autocomplete, five response-card types, FSM placeholders, effective cancellation, and zero “thinking...” messages. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:84)_
- `B14-E17`: Original touch set names commands, ChatWindow, SlashPalette, InterventionCard (possibly reuse B-5), new intent classifier, and `workflow/reasoning/B-14-decisions.md`; V2 rebaseline supersedes V1 frontend paths. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:85)_
- `B14-E18`: Part II gate claims B-11..B-14 collectively deliver design-spec appearance, Workspace primary with in-shell status and 48px/five-icon Sidebar, Activity as centerpiece with auto-scroll, preview links, and collapsible reasoning, plus real-time chat—fully resolving the cited UX complaint before Part III hardening. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:86)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0087`: section=`B-14 - Live token-streaming chat - DONE` V2 Tauri exposes `stream_chat(message)`; chat UI is `CommandWindow.svelte` plus `CommandPalette.svelte`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:171)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0088`: section=`B-14 - Live token-streaming chat - DONE` Frontend token streaming uses Tauri 2 events through `listen` from `@tauri-apps/api/event`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:172)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0089`: section=`B-14 - Live token-streaming chat - DONE` Intent classification is deterministic first with an LLM fallback and returns `TASK`, `FIX`, `EXPLORE`, `CONFIG`, `INTERVENE`, `STEER`, or `ABORT`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:173)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0090`: section=`B-14 - Live token-streaming chat - DONE` Card variants are status, intervention, error, disambiguation, steering, and user; CTA language uses outlined buttons only. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:174)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0091`: section=`B-14 - Live token-streaming chat - DONE` Input placeholder changes by FSM state; Stop calls `cancel_stream`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:175)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0092`: section=`B-14 - Live token-streaming chat - DONE` Token buffer and subscription cleanup use Svelte 5 `$state` and `$effect` respectively. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:176)_

- `B005-F02-003`: — liveness scope (lines 31-46): verify production firing for <code>GroundingSweepGate</code>, <code>SearchMode</code> cascade, <code>SupervisionManager</code>, CSQ pre-tool enforcement, EXECUTING inline feasibility, Auditor anti-rubber-stamp, and <code>CliInteractiveGate</code>; this is a meta-step that adds regression protection and frequency visibility rather than merely defining schemas. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:80)_
- `B005-F02-004`: — liveness harness contract (lines 56-58): add a synthetic-flow suite in <code>gate_liveness.rs</code>; every exercised gate must fire at least once on non-vacuous input. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:81)_
- `B005-F02-005`: — grounding fixture (lines 59-61): an intentionally ungrounded claim must make <code>GroundingSweepGate</code> BLOCK rather than vacuously pass. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:82)_
- `B005-F02-006`: — degradation fixture (lines 62-63): disabling GraphCodeBERT mid-session must move <code>SearchMode</code> to <code>SparseOnly</code> and emit <code>knowledge_degradation</code>. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:83)_
- `B005-F02-007`: — supervision fixture (lines 64-65): an agent crashing three times must trigger <code>SupervisionManager</code> escalation. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:84)_
- `B005-F02-008`: — CSQ fixture (line 66): a destructive call without a guard must be blocked by a Critical CSQ. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:85)_
- `B005-F02-009`: — feasibility fixture (line 67): an injected infeasible inline claim must emit <code>ReplanRequest</code>. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:86)_
- `B005-F02-010`: — anti-rubber-stamp fixture (lines 68-69): an Auditor <code>Converge</code> without a <code>FINDING</code> must emit <code>anti_rubber_stamp_rejected</code>. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:87)_
- `B005-F02-011`: — verdict-count panel (lines 71-79): aggregate over the last N sessions and show GroundingSweep PASS/BLOCK, SearchMode transitions, supervision spawns/escalations, CSQ evaluated/blocked, feasibility PASS/FAIL/BLOCKED, and Auditor converges/rejections. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:88)_
- `B005-F02-012`: — CI enforcement (lines 81-82): run gate liveness on every pull request and fail the build if any gate becomes vacuous. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:89)_
- `B005-F02-013`: — unresolved dismissal policy (lines 84-87): <code>B-17-decisions.md</code> must choose whether dismissing an INTERACTIVE block logs and re-prompts or is treated as Reject. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:90)_
- `B005-F02-014`: — performance gate (lines 89-91): active-gate overhead must remain below 100 ms per FSM transition; if exceeded, optimize, with asynchronous <code>ground_plan</code> parallel to other PLANNING work given as an example. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:91)_
- `B005-F02-015`: — spec lifecycle (lines 93-95): if wiring changes verdict semantics, amend the specification rather than leaving old pass behavior documented. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:92)_
- `B005-F02-016`: — concrete produced surfaces (lines 97-102): the wiring contract records <code>crates/orchestrator/tests/gate_liveness.rs</code> with six active/two ignored tests, an inline <code>VERDICT COUNTS</code> block at <code>OrchestrationDrawer.svelte:418-421</code>, and <code>.github/workflows/gate-liveness.yml</code> plus <code>scripts/check_gate_liveness.sh</code>. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:93)_
- `B005-F02-017`: — panel ownership correction (lines 100-102, 127-129): B-17 deliberately does not create a separate <code>GatesPanel.svelte</code>; that filename belongs to B-38 ambiguity work. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:94)_
- `B005-F02-018`: — concrete reachability (lines 103-110): consume gates from B-2/B-4/B-6/B-8; run CI on push and pull request; dashboard reads <code>stub.get_gate_verdict_counts → commands::get_gate_verdict_counts → ssb_viewer::aggregate_gate_verdict_counts</code>; vacuous-pass-only regression is banned. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:95)_

- `B005-F02-021`: — acceptance gates (lines 132-138): require at least one test per gate and at least six tests, all passing active tests, rendered per-gate counts, PR CI, and transition overhead below 100 ms. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:98)_

### B-18: Tier-C sandbox activation
- **Status:** planned
- **Anchors:** `B18-001` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:98; `B18-002` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:99; `B18-003` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:100; `B18-004` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:101; `B18-005` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:102; `B18-006` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:103; `B18-007` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:104; `B18-008` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:105; `B18-009` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:106; `B18-010` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:107
- **Composes with:**
  - `B-006/INT-002` ↔ B-19: : B-18 supplies the IPC accept/frame path that makes IpcHookDispatcher constructible/live in B-19; B-19 nonetheless retains the in-process implementation as default (B18 lines 81-88; B19 lines 17-20, 34-50).
  - `B-006/INT-003` ↔ B-21: : B-18’s Tier-C process is the required execution boundary for B-21 feasibility stubs (B18 lines 40-89; B21 lines 1-8, 51-53).
  - `B-006/INT-004` ↔ B-23: : B-18 introduces four self-modification enforcement layers; B-23 makes the same dispatch/IPC/filesystem/git set a hard invariant and hides the lock list (B18 lines 102-105; B23 lines 44-49, 77-81).
  - `B-006/INT-019` ↔ B-16, B-21, B-23, B-35: : B-16’s error/fallback doctrine appears concretely as B-18’s weaker Windows fallback, B-21’s explicit hard kill/escalation, B-23’s reject/pause/revert paths, and B-35’s MalformedGrounding/nonfatal artifact failure behavior.
  - `B-006/INT-021` ↔ B-23, B-35: : Security boundaries compose rather than replace one another: B-18 controls agent OS/process/path visibility, B-23 controls self-modifiable state, and B-35 controls artifact file roots. None claims to subsume the others.
  - `B-006/INT-022` ↔ B-23, B-35: : Compliance-aware planning in B-35 is only future groundwork; B-18/B-23 supply isolation and modification controls, but this chunk specifies no certification, audit report, retention policy, or regulatory compliance guarantee (B35 lines 58
  - `B18-001` → B-1, B-15, B-19, B-2, B-20: Scope/status: The target is real Tier-C agent-process isolation plus non-disclosing visibility-filter semantics; B-1, B-2, and B-15 are prerequisites and B-19/B-20 are declared parallel-safe (lines 1-
- **Superseded:** `SUP-005` from `B20-001`: Original objective was one dispatcher and nine failures, dependent on B-18/B-19; the dated 2026-05-23 rebaseline supersedes both simplifications: coexistence remains until B-18B, and C-33 makes twelve categories canonical. (B20:L1-14)
- `B18-001`: Scope/status: The target is real Tier-C agent-process isolation plus non-disclosing visibility-filter semantics; B-1, B-2, and B-15 are prerequisites and B-19/B-20 are declared parallel-safe (lines 1-8). This file has no shipped overlay and every acceptance row remains unchecked (lines 169-179). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:98)_
- `B18-002`: Baseline gap: Existing execution uses a separate process but plain Command::spawn with shared resources; effective isolation is called A++, not Tier C (lines 10-25). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:99)_
- `B18-003`: Dormant Windows implementation: WindowsAppContainerSandbox already contains Win32 calls, but enable_sandbox defaults false and launch_agent_process never calls sandbox.launch_agent (lines 16-18). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:100)_
- `B18-004`: Missing IPC runtime: IpcGateway types exist without an orchestrator UnixListener/NamedPipeServer accept loop (lines 19-20). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:101)_
- `B18-005`: Linux namespace spawn: Linux launch must use clone3 with CLONE_NEWNS, CLONE_NEWPID, and CLONE_NEWNET, exposing only a per-agent temporary root with project source and minimal libraries/tools, hiding other agents, and denying network by default (lines 40-49). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:102)_
- `B18-006`: Researcher network exception: The Researcher receives a dedicated bridged network namespace; its exact bridge configuration is left to the decision record (lines 48-49, 195-200). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:103)_
- `B18-007`: Seccomp profiles: IPC_BASELINE allows required file/IPC/runtime syscalls while denying execution, socket, and network calls; NETWORK_EXTRA permits AF_INET for the Researcher (lines 51-53). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:104)_
- `B18-008`: Privilege prevention: PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS blocks setuid privilege escalation and all Linux capabilities are dropped from the bounding set after clone (lines 55-56, 64-65). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:105)_
- `B18-009`: Resource bounds: Each agent receives CPU limits of 60 seconds soft/90 seconds hard, 2GB memory, 256 files, and 32 processes (lines 58-59). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:106)_
- `B18-010`: Process privacy: The agent mount namespace mounts /proc with hidepid=2 so process information for other agents is unavailable (lines 61-62). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:107)_
- `B18-011`: Windows default: enable_sandbox must default true at the three named production entry points (lines 67-70). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:108)_
- `B18-012`: Windows launch path: launch_agent_process must call self.sandbox.launch_agent and create an AppContainer profile per agent_id (lines 72-76). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:109)_
- `B18-013`: Windows recovery fallback: If AppContainer creation fails on older Windows, use the existing, explicitly weaker restricted-token plus Job-object fallback (lines 78-79). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:110)_
- `B18-014`: IPC endpoint lifecycle: Orchestrator binds /tmp/elai-{session_id}.sock on Linux or the elai-{session_id} named pipe on Windows, agents connect, and IpcGateway routes frames (lines 81-85). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:111)_
- `B18-015`: Hook interception: IpcHookDispatcher must become reachable through IPC frame interception rather than remain dead code (lines 87-88). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:112)_
- `B18-016`: Non-disclosing file errors: A hidden path must return NotFound with “No such file or directory” or the platform equivalent, indistinguishable from a genuinely absent path; error_code = visibility_filtered is forbidden because it leaks filtering (lines 90-96). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:113)_
- `B18-017`: Per-agent authorization: AgentHandle gains allowed_paths: Vec<GlobPattern>; each role has its own glob list instead of relying only on global HIDDEN_PATH_PATTERNS (lines 98-100). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:114)_
- `B18-018`: Self-mod protection: Self-modification uses four gates—IPC, filesystem, git, and dispatch—and Auditor findings may reach PROPOSED only (lines 102-105). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:115)_
- `B18-019`: OS-evidenced tests: Isolation tests must spawn a real agent and inspect /proc status NSpid on Linux or NtQueryInformationProcess on Windows; constructing a struct and checking a field is expressly rejected as fake evidence (lines 107-113). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:116)_
- `B18-020`: Real metrics: phase_4A.toml must measure socket/pipe round-trip latency, steady-state agent RSS, and cold spawn-to-first-handshake latency, replacing postcard serialization, struct size, and HashMap insertion proxies (lines 115-121). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:117)_
- `B18-021`: Wiring/reachability: Produced surfaces are clone3, seccomp loading, invoked Windows launch, the IPC accept loop, and per-agent allowed_paths; consumers are IpcGateway, IpcHookDispatcher, and the visibility filter; both dashboard submit and CLI spawn must reach them (lines 123-147). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:118)_
- `B18-022`: Production bans: Production enable_sandbox=false, struct-only isolation tests, visibility_filtered errors, and proxy phase_4A metrics are prohibited (lines 138-142). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:119)_
- `B18-023`: Observable proof: A dashboard agent spawn must yield an OS fact: a distinct NSpid relationship on Linux or an AppContainer SID on Windows (lines 143-147). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:120)_
- `B18-024`: Done gates: Static checks require no false sandbox default, clone3/seccomp symbols, at least one IPC accept loop, and zero visibility_filtered text in tools; acceptance additionally requires both OS paths, live hook dispatch, allowed paths, real metrics, real cross-platform tests, and a dashboard sandbox badge (lines 149-179). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:121)_
- `B18-025`: Dependency decisions: nix and windows-sys may be added only with confirmation; clone3 versus unshare-then-exec, Linux-only versus workspace-wide nix, and Researcher bridging are explicitly unresolved (lines 181-200). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:122)_
- `B18-026`: Lifecycle: Planned file work spans agent_pool, sandbox, ipc_gateway/listener, dashboard/task-runner defaults, tools visibility, integration tests, phase_4A metrics, and B-18-decisions; B-19 is next (lines 181-203). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:123)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0112`: section=`B-18 - Tier-C sandbox activation - PLANNED` Linux isolation uses `clone3` with `CLONE_NEWNS|CLONE_NEWPID|CLONE_NEWNET`; a seccomp-bpf IPC baseline plus researcher-only network additions; `PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS`; limits of 60 seconds soft CPU, 2GB memory, 256 files, and 32 processes; `/proc hidepid=2`; and capability bounding-set drop. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:216)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0113`: section=`B-18 - Tier-C sandbox activation - PLANNED` Windows enables `enable_sandbox=true` at three entry points, launches via `self.sandbox.launch_agent()`, creates one AppContainer profile per `agent_id`, and falls back to a restricted token plus Job object. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:217)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0114`: section=`B-18 - Tier-C sandbox activation - PLANNED` IPC accept loop is `UnixListener` on Linux or `NamedPipeServer` on Windows; frames pass through `IpcGateway`; dead `IpcHookDispatcher` wiring becomes live. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:218)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0115`: section=`B-18 - Tier-C sandbox activation - PLANNED` Visibility filtering must return NotFound semantics instead of `error_code="visibility_filtered"`; each `AgentHandle` owns `allowed_paths`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:219)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0116`: section=`B-18 - Tier-C sandbox activation - PLANNED` Self-modification is protected at dispatch, IPC, filesystem, and git layers. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:220)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0117`: section=`B-18 - Tier-C sandbox activation - PLANNED` Replace fake namespace tests with real `/proc/{pid}/status` `NSpid` checks. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:221)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0118`: section=`B-18 - Tier-C sandbox activation - PLANNED` `phase_4A.toml` must contain real isolation/IPC metrics, not an in-process postcard round-trip mislabeled as IPC latency. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:222)_

- `B005-F03-010`: — mode state (lines 70-71, 117-118): <code>/mode autonomous|interactive</code> is per-session, not global, and takes effect at the next gate. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:125)_
- `B005-F03-011`: — tree auto-advance policy (lines 72-80, 120-122): advance at BabyStep granularity; risk is BabyStep LOW, Step LOW, Phase MEDIUM, Concept HIGH; Concept bubble-up always requires a human; force a human check after fifty auto baby steps per concept; represent this as <code>TreeAutoAdvancePolicy</code>. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:126)_

- `B005-F03-018`: — deterministic/acceptance gates (lines 164-191): require a concrete <code>AutonomousGate</code>, at least 36 autonomous tests, <code>SlashCommand::Mode</code>, all six checks/four triggers, bounded split/model substitution, functional mode/model commands, model-selection UI, level risk, both breakers, MonitorWindow stats, and 36+ passing tests. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:133)_

- `B005-F03-021`: — Spinning detector (lines 212-218): <code>Spinning</code> fires on at least three identical <code>phase_outputs</code> or <code>error_signatures</code>, compared by blake3; it subsumes the old same-step rule. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:136)_
- `B005-F03-022`: — Oscillation detector (lines 212-218): <code>Oscillation</code> recognizes period-two alternation and requires at least four outputs. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:137)_
- `B005-F03-023`: — NoDrift detector (lines 212-218): <code>NoDrift</code> fires when absolute drift delta is below 0.01 for three iterations. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:138)_
- `B005-F03-024`: — DiminishingReturns detector (lines 212-218): <code>DiminishingReturns</code> requires improvements below 0.01 that are also monotonically decreasing. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:139)_
- `B005-F03-025`: — detector purity (lines 217-218): <code>StagnationDetector</code> is stateless over <code>ExecutionHistory</code>. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:140)_
- `B005-F03-026`: — persona affinity (lines 220-225): Hacker maps to Spinning; Researcher to NoDrift/DiminishingReturns; Simplifier to DiminishingReturns/Oscillation; Architect to Oscillation/NoDrift; Contrarian to all patterns. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:141)_
- `B005-F03-027`: — persona selection/exhaustion (lines 226-228): <code>suggest_persona_for_pattern(pattern, exclude)</code> returns the first priority match; exhaustion emits <code>lateral_thinking_exhausted</code> and follows existing <code>EscalationPolicy</code>. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:142)_
- `B005-F03-028`: — optional fan-out (lines 230-232): an optional flag may spawn all five personas in parallel through Tier C, each with fresh context to avoid anchoring; first success wins. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:143)_
- `B005-F03-029`: — watchdog timers (lines 234-238): per BabyStep <code>GenerationWatchdog</code> defaults are 60 seconds with no SSB event, 180 seconds with events but no material progress, and a 600-second wall-clock safety cap. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:144)_
- `B005-F03-030`: — watchdog materiality/dedup (lines 239-243): material events are <code>phase_transition</code>, <code>evidence_gate_evaluated</code>, <code>integration_checkpoint_success</code>, <code>bubble_up_typed</code>, and state-changing <code>agent_lifecycle</code>; heartbeats/status echoes do not count; <code>workflow_fingerprint</code> dedup blocks replay gaming. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:145)_
- `B005-F03-031`: — persona artifact set (lines 245-248): add <code>persona-hacker.md</code>, <code>persona-researcher.md</code>, <code>persona-simplifier.md</code>, <code>persona-architect.md</code>, and <code>persona-contrarian.md</code> under <code>.claude/agents/</code>, following existing agent-file format. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:146)_
- `B005-F03-032`: — goal-drift calculation (lines 250-253): calculate <code>drift = 1 - jaccard(tokens(output), tokens(baby_step.goal))</code> using lowercase alphanumeric tokens split on whitespace. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:147)_
- `B005-F03-033`: — goal-drift verdict/recovery (lines 254-256): drift below 0.5 passes; 0.5–0.7 emits an advisory SSB warning and continues to audit; at least 0.7 blocks before audit and causes <code>BubbleUp RetryBabyStep</code> with at most one retry. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:148)_
- `B005-F03-034`: — goal-drift skip/economy (lines 257-259): skip if output has fewer than twenty tokens or goal is empty; every Block is intended to save at least one audit model call and must be measured in 4G.6 metrics. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:149)_
- `B005-F03-035`: — goal-drift non-goals/provenance (lines 260-270): do not port <code>constraint_drift</code> or <code>ontology_drift</code> because hooks and OSC cover them; port only the stated MIT-licensed ouroboros stagnation, lateral, watchdog, goal-drift, and multi-persona patterns. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:150)_
- `B005-F03-036`: — follow-up effort/downstream (lines 272-277): B-22b is about two sessions—one for patterns/watchdog and one for fan-out/persona files/canary—then B-23 self-modification follows. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:151)_

- `B23-006`: Parent selection: L2 uses sigmoid-weighted DGM parent selection with lambda = 10 and alpha_0 = 0.5 (lines 25-27, 69-70). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:193)_
- `B23-007`: Log-driven proposals: L2 reads SSB and reasoning_trace, identifies where reasoning failed, and proposes a config patch (lines 71-72). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:194)_
- `B23-008`: Staged evaluation: Evaluation progresses through 10, 50, and 200 tasks; more than 40% passing and the top two variants promote to Stage 3 (lines 27-28, 73-74). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:195)_
- `B23-009`: Outcome grading: TestOutcomeReport uses four SWE-bench categories and PASS_TO_FAIL causes immediate rejection; SWR pre-screening is optional (lines 27-28, 75-76). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:196)_
- `B23-010`: L3 non-goal: Structural FSM-state, agent-role, or GRC-protocol changes are HUMAN-ONLY stubs; automation may emit l3_proposal for review but may not auto-promote (lines 29, 44-46, 85-86). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:197)_
- `B23-011`: MSI calculation: MsiTracker computes an exponential-decay weighted average of delta_score with lambda = 0.3 over N = 10, maintaining independent SURGICAL and GREENFIELD histories (lines 34-36, 79). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:198)_
- `B23-012`: MSI state machine: IMPROVING > 0.3 continues; STABILIZING from -0.1 through 0.3 reduces modification frequency; DIVERGING <= -0.1 pauses and alerts (lines 37-39). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:199)_
- `B23-013`: Auto-revert: More than 10% degradation across three pipelines triggers revert; a trigger task checks objective hacking (lines 41-42). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:200)_
- `B23-014`: Mutable-key authorization: AllowedConfigKey is a closed enum; cloud_budget, gate definitions, and score weights are locked, and adding a mutable key requires Layer-3 human approval (lines 44-46, 79-81). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:201)_
- `B23-015`: Four enforcement layers: Dispatch, IPC, filesystem, and git independently enforce locks; the modifying agent cannot read the lock list (lines 48-49, 77-78). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:202)_
- `B23-016`: Anti-stale integration: Nightly dependency audit can grow deprecated_patterns and trigger steering-vector refresh (lines 51-52, 82-83). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:203)_
- `B23-017`: Reasoning integration: failure_taxonomy_misses feed L1A category growth (lines 53, 84). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:204)_
- `B23-018`: Dashboard activation: ProposalsPanel is activated behind a feature flag and shows archive, per-mode MSI, pending proposals, staged results, and last revert; MsiPanel shows mode verdict and convergence/regression/evolution badges (lines 87-89, 120-123, 165-171). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:205)_
- `B23-019`: OSC similarity: ConfigVariantSimilarity computes key_overlap × 0.5 + type_match × 0.3 + exact_match × 0.2 over deserialized config_blob (lines 91-94). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:206)_
- `B23-020`: OSC stagnation: Similarity >= 0.95 for three consecutive variants marks Stagnant and boosts exploration, independently per mode (lines 94-98). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:207)_
- `B23-021`: OSC period-two proof: Oscillation requires both sim(N,N-2) and sim(N-1,N-3) >= 0.95; differing keys are excluded from AllowedConfigKey at runtime for five pipelines (lines 95-98). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:208)_
- `B23-022`: Cross-pipeline regression: RegressionDetector examines the full PipelineOutcome history, unlike single-evaluation PASS_TO_FAIL; persistent criteria that never passed are not regressions (lines 102-110). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:209)_
- `B23-023`: Regression control flow: A detected regression blocks 4H.5 promotion despite an improved score and three consecutive regressions on the active variant trigger 4H.6 auto-revert, per mode (lines 104-109). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:210)_
- `B23-024`: Evolution gate: evolved_count is the count of adjacent variant pairs with similarity < 0.95; when it is zero and latest similarity >= 0.95, evolution is withheld and that mode’s MSI is forced to STABILIZING (lines 112-118). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:211)_
- `B23-025`: Convergence events: Four new rows are convergence_stagnant, config_oscillation, regression_detected, and evolution_withheld, surfaced beside per-mode MSI (lines 120-123). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:212)_
- `B23-026`: Wiring symbols: The implementation contract names DefaultLayer1aProcessor, SelfModOrchestrator, ConfigArchive, evaluate_stage, run_full_pipeline, TestOutcomeReport, MsiTracker, AllowedConfigKey, is_locked, check_locked_key, should_auto_revert, ConfigVariantSimilarity, OscVerdict/detect, RegressionReport, compute_evolution_gate, and EvolutionGateResult (lines 125-144). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:213)_
- `B23-027`: Base event naming/count: The wiring contract explicitly names ProposalGenerated, ProposalApplied, ProposalRejected, MsiUpdate, DataTableGrew, and L3Proposal plus four convergence events—ten named variants (lines 145-154). The files section instead says 11 new action types comprising seven base plus four convergence events (lines 213-215), leaving one base variant unnamed in this source. _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:214)_
- `B23-028`: Reachability: The self-mod loop is reached from autonomous B-22 and a nightly cron; PipelineOutcome and ProposalsPanel are consumers (lines 155-160). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:215)_
- `B23-029`: Hard safety bans: Locked keys must never be mutable, PASS_TO_FAIL must reject immediately, and DIVERGING MSI must pause and alert (lines 161-165). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:216)_
- `B23-030`: Observable evidence: The Layer1a SSB smoke must produce msi_update; the UI must show an archive capped at 50 and independent IMPROVING/STABILIZING/DIVERGING modes (lines 166-171). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:217)_
- `B23-031`: Deterministic checks: Static AllowedConfigKey discovery plus at least 42 self-mod tests are the minimal scripted checks (lines 173-181). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:218)_
- `B23-032`: Acceptance expansion: The original 13-substep/42-test scope was expanded by OSC/REG/EVO to 54+ tests and four additional events, while every acceptance box remains unchecked (lines 183-203). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:219)_
- `B23-033`: Acceptance gates: Required outcomes include functional L1/L1A/L2, 50-variant archives, 10/50/200 staging, four-category grading, per-mode MSI, closed keys, four locks, anti-stale/reasoning wiring, L3 proposal-only behavior, live panels, OSC period-two validation, REG promotion/revert control, and EVO forced stabilization (lines 183-203). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:220)_
- `B23-034`: Artifact lifecycle: Planned artifacts are self_mod.rs, config_archive.rs, msi.rs, osc.rs, regression.rs, evolution_gate.rs, SSB changes, ProposalsPanel/MsiPanel, optional self_mod_cron.sh, and B-23-decisions (lines 205-223). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:221)_
- `B23-035`: Dashboard API compatibility: Existing stub names get_pending_proposals, get_applied_proposals, apply_proposal_from_user, reject_proposal, and get_msi_status must be retained one-to-one when V2 wiring lands (lines 216-221). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:222)_
- `B23-036`: Lifecycle: B-24 media validation follows (lines 225-227). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:223)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0158`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` L1 collects automatic metrics without an LLM. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:287)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0159`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` L1A automatically grows additive data-table rows without an LLM or staged evaluation in `deprecated_patterns`, `FailureTaxonomy`, lazy-code regex, and source-tier; this is the DarwinNet Gene Pool lane. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:288)_

- `SUMMARY/SUM-0161`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` `TestOutcomeReport` uses four-category SWE-bench grading; `PASS_TO_FAIL` rejects immediately. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:290)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0162`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` L3 structural change remains HUMAN-ONLY stubs and emits `l3_proposal`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:291)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0163`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` Modification-Solidification Index uses exponential decay `lambda=0.3`, `N=10` per PipelineMode. `IMPROVING >0.3` continues; `STABILIZING -0.1..0.3` reduces; `DIVERGING <=-0.1` pauses and alerts. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:292)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0164`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` Auto-revert occurs after more than 10% degradation across three pipelines. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:293)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0165`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` `AllowedConfigKey` is a closed enum; cloud budget, gate definitions, and score weights are locked and never mutable. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:294)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0166`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` Four independent safety enforcement layers are dispatch, IPC, filesystem, and git; the self-mod agent cannot read the lock list. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:295)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0167`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` OSC/4H.7B similarity is `0.5*key_overlap + 0.3*type_match + 0.2*exact_match`; score >=0.95 across three means stagnant and boosts exploration; period-two oscillation triggers `PERIOD-2 FULL VERIFICATION` and freezes the involved keys. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:296)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0168`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` REG/4H.7C scans full `PipelineOutcome` history, blocks 4H.5 promotion, and triggers 4H.6 auto-revert after three consecutive regressed pipelines. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:297)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0169`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` EVO/4H.7D detects a false IMPROVING label when MSI changes due to noise while `config_blob` is structurally identical; promotion is withheld and MSI forced to STABILIZING. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:298)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0170`: section=`B-23 - Phase 4H self-modification (DGM/CEL/DarwinNet) - PLANNED` Eleven events: base `self_mod_proposed`, `self_mod_evaluated`, `self_mod_promoted`, `self_mod_reverted`, `msi_state_changed`, `data_table_grew`, `l3_proposal`, plus `convergence_stagnant`, `config_oscillation`, `regression_detected`, `evolution_withheld` (lines 967-978). ### B-27: Cloud mode (LOCAL/CLOUD/HYBRID)
- **Status:** planned
- **Anchors:** `B27-E01` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:134; `B27-E02` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:135; `B27-E03` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:136; `B27-E04` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:137; `B27-E05` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:138; `B27-E06` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:139; `B27-E07` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:140; `B27-E08` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:141; `B27-E09` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:142; `B27-E10` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:143
- **Composes with:**
  - `B-004/X-E04` ↔ B-25: B-25 mounts EmpireBridgePanel in SettingsWindow between runtime and cloud; B-27 puts CloudPanel/BYOE/spend configuration in SettingsWindow’s cloud area (B-25 L117-L122, L196-L202; B-27 L232-L245).
  - `B-004/X-E05` ↔ B-10: B-27’s cloud mode is explicitly another B-10 ContextBudget profile and is gated on B-10 activation (B-27 L1-L10, L108-L117).
  - `B-004/X-I03` ↔ B-25: SettingsWindow’s likely section sequence is runtime -> Empire -> cloud, combining B-25’s explicit insertion point with B-27’s CloudPanel ownership. Responsive/layout ownership is not defined by these files.
  - `B-004/X-I09` ↔ B-36: B-27’s pre-call redaction and spend gate should wrap any cloud-backed execution reached from dashboard commands, but B-36’s D.7 subprocess environment does not explicitly call `CloudModeRouter` or `RedactingBabyStepExecutor`.
  - `B-004/X-A01` ↔ B-25, B-36, B-42: B-27 air-gap disables all external network, but B-36 assumes a remote sidecar, B-42 schedules web research, and B-25 supports TCP Empire. The precedence/gating contract for those three integrations under `air_gapped=true` is unstated.
  - `B-004/X-A03` ↔ B-25, B-42: B-27 privacy redaction covers cloud model context, but whether B-42 FlashResearch queries/results and B-25 Empire event payloads pass the same redactor/audit trail is not stated.
- `B27-E01`: Per-task modes are `LOCAL` (default, full local pipeline, all six reasoning layers, $0), `CLOUD` (cloud generation, deterministic L1 still runs, L2-L5 skipped), and `HYBRID` (local first; only the failing L3+ step escalates). _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:134)_
- `B27-E02`: Routing reuses the providers crate. `CLOUD_SPEND` records cost; per-task and per-day ceilings and pre-task estimation are mandatory. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:135)_
- `B27-E03`: Cloud versus local: context 200K vs 8-25K; baby step 200-500 LOC vs 25-40; depth 2 vs 4; phase split 50K tokens vs 3K; telescope optional vs mandatory. Cloud slots are `system_prompt=500`, `rules=200`, `phase_spec=2000`, `repo_map=4000`, `code_context=150000`, `ssb_entries=2000`, `grc_history=1000`, `output_reserve=8000`. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:136)_
- `B27-E04`: `redact_env_vars` defaults true; `redact_patterns` is a regex list; `blocked_paths` is a glob list. Every cloud call audits timestamp, provider/model, token count, cost, included files, and redaction count. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:137)_
- `B27-E05`: BYOE accepts an API-compatible endpoint such as vLLM/TGI/Ollama on user-owned hardware. Air-gapped mode explicitly disables all external networking; nightly dependency audit, cloud escalation, and Oracle web search degrade off while core operation continues. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:138)_
- `B27-E06`: On local Layer 3+ failure, emit `ESCALATE_TO_CLOUD`, switch provider for that step only, then continue; D-3 redaction applies to escalated context. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:139)_
- `B27-E07`: fields/defaults are `provider="anthropic"`, `model="claude-sonnet-4-6"`, `escalation_model="claude-opus-4-6"`, `api_key_env="ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"`, `max_spend_per_task_usd=2.00`, `max_spend_per_day_usd=10.00`, `warn_at_percent=80`; `[execution].default_mode="local"`. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:140)_
- `B27-E08`: Routing steps are read config, verify API key, probe model availability with a <100ms tokenize operation, use `[cloud.fallback]` if probe fails, and degrade to LOCAL with a notification when no fallback exists. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:141)_
- `B27-E09`: `CLOUD_SPEND` accumulates per task and per day and enforces both ceilings. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:142)_
- `B27-E10`: `/task --mode cloud|hybrid|local` selects mode; `/config` changes the default; session metadata gains `execution_mode`. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:143)_
- `B27-E11`: implements all eight specified slots. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:144)_
- `B27-E12`: Baby-step LOC values are `local_3b=25`, `local_7b=40`, `local_32b=100`, `cloud_sonnet=300`, and `cloud_opus=500`; maximum decomposition depth is LOCAL 4 and CLOUD 2. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:145)_
- `B27-E13`: CLOUD runs L1 deterministic plus L6 generation, skipping L2-L5. On L1 failure, inject findings, retry at most three times, then use `escalation_model`. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:146)_
- `B27-E14`: HYBRID runs L1-L4 locally and makes one cloud call on L3+ failure. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:147)_
- `B27-E15`: includes `allow_source_upload=true`, `allow_test_upload=true`, `redact_env_vars=true`, regex patterns (example API-key assignment), and blocked defaults `.env`, `*.pem`, `*.key`, `credentials.*`. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:148)_
- `B27-E16`: Redaction runs before context assembly and substitutes `[REDACTED:type]`. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:149)_
- `B27-E17`: Cloud-call audit stores timestamp, provider/model, token counts, cost, file paths only (never content), and redaction count. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:150)_
- `B27-E18`: BYOE is `[cloud] provider="custom", custom_endpoint=URL` through the OpenAI-compatible adapter. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:151)_
- `B27-E19`: air_gapped=true` disables all external network and requires graceful feature disablement. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:152)_
- `B27-E20`: `ESCALATE_TO_CLOUD` records L3+ failure escalation, limits provider switch to the current step, and preserves privacy redaction. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:153)_
- `B27-E21`: Estimate is `phase_count * avg_tokens * pricing`; user sees a range such as `$0.12-$0.35` and must answer Proceed `[y/n]`. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:154)_
- `B27-E22`: Monitor status shows running per-task spend including `$0.00`. At default 80%, COMMAND receives a warning; at 100%, HYBRID degrades and CLOUD fails the step. CloudPanel controls provider/model and air-gap, and shows per-task/per-day gauges plus redaction statistics. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:155)_
- `B27-E23`: Produced orchestrator symbols are `CloudModeRouter`, `CloudConfig`, `ExecMode`, `RoutedProvider`, `SpendAccumulator`, `RedactingBabyStepExecutor`, `estimate_task_cost_usd`, `resolve_exec_mode`, `baby_step_loc_for_mode`, `route_with_spend`, `escalate_hybrid_step_to_cloud`, and `prompt_cost_estimate_gate`; privacy symbols are `redact_context`/`PrivacyConfig`; context is `cloud_200k`; SSB types are `CloudSpend`, `EscalateToCloud`, `RouteBlocked`, `CostEstimate`, `WarnLimitReached`, `RedactionApplied`, `ExecutionMod… _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:156)_
- `B27-E24`: Consumers/reachability are `runtime_setup::apply_task_runner_wiring`, `task_runner::start_task_runner`, `run_subcommand::run_async`, `task_cmd::drive_task`, and dashboard `submit_task`. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:157)_
- `B27-E25`: A cloud executor without redaction is prohibited; `route_with_spend` must enforce declared caps; `redact_context` must scrub environment-token secrets. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:158)_
- `B27-E26`: Smoke evidence is `cloud_spend` plus dashboard running spend; static checks require config schema, `CloudSpend`, and orchestrator privacy-redaction tests. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:159)_
- `B27-E27`: All four D groups, three selectable modes, spend ceilings, cloud profile/sizing/collapse, privacy defaults/BYOE/air-gap, single-call HYBRID escalation, visible estimate, and functional CloudPanel are required. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:160)_
- `B27-E28`: Create `cloud_mode.rs` and `privacy_redactor.rs`; modify context budget, TaskRunner, SSB, CLI flags, V2 Tauri/UI, TOML schemas; create `B-27-decisions.md`. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:161)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0197`: section=`B-27 - Cloud mode - PLANNED` Per-task `LOCAL` is default, costs $0, and uses six reasoning layers. `CLOUD` lets cloud generate while local deterministic L1 still runs and L2-L5 are skipped. `HYBRID` tries local first and escalates only a failing L3+ step. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:346)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0198`: section=`B-27 - Cloud mode - PLANNED` `[cloud]` settings: `provider="anthropic"`, `model="claude-sonnet-4-6"`, `escalation_model="claude-opus-4-6"`, `max_spend_per_task`, `max_spend_per_day`, `warn_at_percent`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:347)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0199`: section=`B-27 - Cloud mode - PLANNED` Emit `CLOUD_SPEND`; enforce ceilings; expose `/task --mode`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:348)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0200`: section=`B-27 - Cloud mode - PLANNED` Cloud ContextBudget profile is 200K versus local 8-25K; cloud babysteps are 300-500 LOC versus local 25-40; decomposition depth is two versus four; split threshold is 50K versus 3K; reasoning layers collapse. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:349)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0201`: section=`B-27 - Cloud mode - PLANNED` Privacy controls: `redact_env_vars` default, regex `redact_patterns`, glob `blocked_paths`; every cloud call gets an audit trail. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:350)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0202`: section=`B-27 - Cloud mode - PLANNED` BYOE uses a custom OpenAI-compatible `custom_endpoint` URL; `[execution] air_gapped=true` disables cloud reachability. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:351)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0203`: section=`B-27 - Cloud mode - PLANNED` Emit `ESCALATE_TO_CLOUD`; estimate cost before the task; show a per-task Monitor spend gauge; warn at 80% and 100%; expose `CloudPanel`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:352)_

- **Anchors:** `B005-F05-001` @ DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:210; `B005-F05-002` @ DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:211; `B005-F05-003` @ DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:212; `B005-F05-004` @ DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:213; `B005-F05-005` @ DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:214; `B005-F05-006` @ DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:215; `B005-F05-007` @ DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:216; `B005-F05-008` @ DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:217; `B005-F05-009` @ DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:218; `B005-F05-010` @ DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:219

  - `B005-F05-021` → B-20: — consumed systems/reachability (lines 141-146): depend on sidecar FIM tokens, B-20 edit matching, and 3F deprecated patterns; execute from <code>task_runner.rs::drive_task</code> for every BabyStep g
  - `B005-F05-026` → B-21: — Part-IV completion gate (lines 194-203): after B-21–B-28, the plan claims Phase 4F–4J/4K post-MVP, cloud, Focus Tunnel, MSI self-modification, conditional Empire integration, and the architecture ov
  - `B005-F05-027` → B-1, B-15, B-19, B-20: — deferred/downstream boundary (lines 205-211): this was the intended end of the original B-plan; Phase 5 Empire deep work and compiled pipelines are deferred, Phase 3L Project Intelligence is recomme
- **Superseded:** `SUP-068` from `B-005/X05`: : B-28 depends on B-20’s three-tier edit matcher and 3F anti-stale patterns, and relies on B-22 for full autonomous benefit; B-26 and B-28 are intentionally parallel-safe (<code>F05:1-9, 74-79, 100-107, 141-150; F04:19, 239-241</code>).
- `B005-F05-001`: — objective/dependencies (lines 1-9): implement Focus Tunnel E-1–E-7—FIM, diff, deterministic mode selection, streaming validation, depth-one preassembly, dependency resolution, and file routing—in three to four sessions; require B-15–B-20 and B-22 for full benefit; allow B-25/B-26/B-27 in parallel. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:210)_
- `B005-F05-002`: — FIM mode (lines 16-22): for a single-region edit in an existing file on native Qwen2.5-Coder, output raw code between prefix/suffix without tool schemas or JSON, saving about 350 tokens per step. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:211)_
- `B005-F05-003`: — DIFF mode (lines 20-22): multi-region edits use unified diffs applied through B-20’s three-tier matching and are estimated 30–50% smaller than full-file output. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:212)_
- `B005-F05-004`: — STRUCTURED mode (line 22): STRUCTURED remains the default and the fallback for new files and cloud. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:213)_
- `B005-F05-005`: — deterministic selector (lines 24-29): <code>edit_existing + single region + supports_fim</code> selects FIM; <code>edit_existing + multi-region</code> selects DIFF; <code>create_file</code> and every other case select STRUCTURED; no LLM participates. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:214)_
- `B005-F05-006`: — token validation (lines 31-36): check imports against <code>deprecated_patterns</code> and detect lazy code such as <code>todo!</code>/<code>pass</code>; terminate bad output early. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:215)_
- `B005-F05-007`: — chunk validation (lines 31-36): run a partial tree-sitter parse about every fifty tokens and terminate on structural impossibility; catching a bad generation at token 50 instead of 500 is estimated to save about 90%. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:216)_
- `B005-F05-008`: — pipeline preassembly (lines 38-41): while step N validates, preassemble context for N+1; depth is one; expected savings are 100–500 ms on the roughly 88% of steps that validate. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:217)_
- `B005-F05-009`: — plan-owned routing (lines 43-45): <code>target_file</code> comes from the plan, never the model; an AST mismatch creates a file microstep and retries. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:218)_
- `B005-F05-010`: — dependency safety baseline (lines 47-52): infer missing dependencies from imports, check anti-staleness, resolve a version and install it; the model may never run <code>cargo add</code> or <code>pip install</code>; autonomous mode uses a pre-approved allowlist. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:219)_
- `B005-F05-011`: — claimed budget impact (lines 54-55): estimate 6,800 tokens/27% saved on a thirty-step 25K-token task and a 45–65% effective budget increase when combined with ShortCoder. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:220)_
- `B005-F05-012`: — FIM capabilities/prompts (lines 65-72): add <code>ModelCapabilities.supports_fim</code>, true for Qwen2.5-Coder, StarCoder, and DeepSeek-Coder and false for cloud; <code>FimPromptAssembler</code> prefix contains header/imports/referenced symbols/pre-edit code and suffix contains post-edit code plus fifty lines or EOF; insert output at the edit region and run PostToolUse hooks; unsupported models fall back to STRUCTURED. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:221)_
- `B005-F05-013`: — diff parser/recovery (lines 74-79): parse hunks with file/line/added/removed data, apply three-tier matching, support multiple regions, and fall back to STRUCTURED on parse failure. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:222)_
- `B005-F05-014`: — per-step preference/telemetry (lines 81-85): <code>PipelineSpec</code> steps gain <code>preferred_mode: Option&lt;GenerationMode&gt;</code>; SSB records <code>mode_distribution</code> per task. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:223)_
- `B005-F05-015`: — streaming telemetry (lines 87-92): expose <code>early_termination_rate</code> and <code>tokens_saved</code>. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:224)_
- `B005-F05-016`: — preassembly discard/adaptation (lines 94-98): discard N+1 context if N fails because it depends on N’s output; start at depth one and disable if validation pass rate drops below 80%. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:225)_
- `B005-F05-017`: — dependency-resolution pipeline (lines 100-107): identify import → check anti-staleness → query registry → conflict check → edit manifest → install; interactive new dependencies require confirmation, while pre-approved autonomous dependencies and their transitives are auto-approved. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:226)_
- `B005-F05-018`: — speculative microstep recovery (lines 109-118): <code>target_file + edit_region</code> drive FIM/DIFF setup and write destination; undefined-symbol AST evidence produces a MINOR <code>ReplanRequest</code> “create {path} with {type}” in STRUCTURED mode, then retries original work with updated context; cap at two microsteps per BabyStep. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:227)_
- `B005-F05-019`: — dashboard telemetry (lines 120-125): show per-task FIM/DIFF/STRUCTURED percentages, early-termination-rate gauge, preassembly depth/waste rate, and auto-installed dependency version/source log. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:228)_
- `B005-F05-020`: — symbol/event registry (lines 127-140): produce <code>FimPromptAssembler</code>, <code>DiffParser</code>, <code>ModeSelector</code>, <code>StreamingValidator</code>, <code>PipelinePreAssembler</code>, <code>DependencyResolver</code>, <code>SpeculativeFileRouter</code>, <code>GenerationModeSelected</code>, <code>StreamingEarlyTerminated</code>, <code>PipelinePreAssembled</code>, and <code>DependencyResolved</code>. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:229)_
- `B005-F05-021`: — consumed systems/reachability (lines 141-146): depend on sidecar FIM tokens, B-20 edit matching, and 3F deprecated patterns; execute from <code>task_runner.rs::drive_task</code> for every BabyStep generation. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:230)_
- `B005-F05-022`: — hard bans (lines 147-150): disallow direct model package-manager execution, preassembly depth above one, and unconfirmed dependencies in INTERACTIVE mode. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:231)_
- `B005-F05-023`: — observable contract (lines 151-156): dashboard is the frontend pair; the <code>b28_focus_tunnel_smoke_ssb</code> contract must emit <code>generation_mode_selected</code> and show mode distribution plus preassembly stats. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:232)_
- `B005-F05-024`: — deterministic/acceptance gates (lines 158-179): locate FIM support, selector, and dependency resolver; require all seven E deliverables, working FIM/DIFF, deterministic selection, early termination, measurable 100–500 ms parallelism saving, allowlisted dependency automation, file microsteps, and dashboard panels. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:233)_
- `B005-F05-025`: — implementation footprint (lines 181-192): add seven <code>focus_tunnel</code> modules and <code>FocusTunnelPanel.svelte</code>; modify providers/tools/TaskRunner/SSB/V2 Tauri; create <code>B-28-decisions.md</code>. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:234)_
- `B005-F05-026`: — Part-IV completion gate (lines 194-203): after B-21–B-28, the plan claims Phase 4F–4J/4K post-MVP, cloud, Focus Tunnel, MSI self-modification, conditional Empire integration, and the architecture overview complete enough for v1.0. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:235)_
- `B005-F05-027`: — deferred/downstream boundary (lines 205-211): this was the intended end of the original B-plan; Phase 5 Empire deep work and compiled pipelines are deferred, Phase 3L Project Intelligence is recommended after B-1–B-20, and spec amendments are split across B-15/B-19/B-20. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:236)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0204`: section=`B-28 - Focus Tunnel - PLANNED` Deterministic emission mode selection has three modes: `FIM` for single-region edits with native Qwen2.5-Coder fill-in-middle and no tool schemas (~350 tokens saved/step); `DIFF` for multi-region unified diffs, roughly 30-50% smaller than a full file and using B-20 three-tier match; `STRUCTURED` is the default/fallback for new files and cloud. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:358)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0205`: section=`B-28 - Focus Tunnel - PLANNED` `ModelCapabilities.supports_fim` is true for Qwen2.5-Coder, StarCoder, and DeepSeek-Coder, false for cloud; `ModeSelector` applies the canonical spec rules. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:359)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0206`: section=`B-28 - Focus Tunnel - PLANNED` Token-level streaming validation checks imports against `deprecated_patterns`, detects lazy code such as `todo!`/`pass`, and can terminate early. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:360)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0207`: section=`B-28 - Focus Tunnel - PLANNED` Chunk-level validation does a partial tree-sitter parse about every fifty tokens. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:361)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0208`: section=`B-28 - Focus Tunnel - PLANNED` Expected bad-generation saving is approximately 90% when caught at token 50 rather than 500. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:362)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0209`: section=`B-28 - Focus Tunnel - PLANNED` Depth-one pipeline parallelism prepares step N+1 context while step N validates; it disables adaptively if validation pass rate falls below 80%. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:363)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0210`: section=`B-28 - Focus Tunnel - PLANNED` Automated dependency resolution is detect missing import -> anti-staleness check -> registry lookup -> conflict check -> manifest edit -> install. The model never runs `cargo add` or `pip install`; autonomous mode requires a pre-approved allowlist. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:364)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0211`: section=`B-28 - Focus Tunnel - PLANNED` Speculative file routing uses `target_file`; AST mismatch creates a file in a microstep and retries, capped at two microsteps per babystep. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:365)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0212`: section=`B-28 - Focus Tunnel - PLANNED` Claimed estimate: about 6,800 tokens/27% saved on a thirty-step 25K task; with ShortCoder, 45-65% effective budget increase. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:366)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0213`: section=`B-28 - Focus Tunnel - PLANNED` Events: `generation_mode_selected`, `streaming_early_terminated`, `pipeline_pre_assembled`, `dependency_resolved`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:367)_

  - `B-006/INT-013` ↔ B-29.5, B-31, B-31.5: : B-29.5 is an evidence gate over B-29 traversal/cache claims and depends on B-31 resume; B-31.5 shares fixtures but measures an orthogonal indexing domain (B29.5 lines 1-12, 192-209, 458-461).

- `B005-F06-001`: — objective/actors (lines 1-10): add agent-callable blast radius, call flow, symbol context, and symbol list over a sharded/LRU-bounded <code>SymbolGraphCache</code> so Auditor, Designer, and Researcher can reason without loading the whole DKG; estimate 12–18 hours, require B-1, recommend B-34, and allow B-30/B-31/B-32/B-35 in parallel. _(anchor: DEEP-B-005.discovery.md:251)_
