- `B34-001`: Objective: deterministic orphan/name/framework-route detection exposed as a dispatcher tool, grounding Designer recursive decomposition and Researcher <code>/research</code> starting points; estimate 5–7 hours (lines 1-8). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:282)_
- `B34-002`: Requires B-29 <code>SymbolGraphCache</code>/per-file payloads and is parallel-safe with B-30/B-31/B-32/B-33/B-35 (lines 9-11). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:283)_
- `B34-003`: Existing Designer <code>EXISTING_PROJECT</code> flow otherwise hallucinates structure or mistakes filename matches for genuine entry points; Researcher has only file/symbol search and lacks routes/CLI starts (lines 13-30). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:284)_
- `B34-004`: Algorithm is LLM-free and attaches a reason string, so consumers see why each entry was classified (lines 32-42). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:285)_
- `B34-005`: <code>entry_point_names_per_language()</code> returns an ordered BTreeMap for 14 languages (lines 61-86). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:286)_
- `B34-006`: Exact lowercase-<code>main</code> languages are JavaScript, TypeScript, Python, Go, Rust, Java, Kotlin, Scala, C, C++, Swift, Ruby, and PHP; C# uses uppercase <code>Main</code>; Python separately recognizes <code>__main__</code> (lines 66-86). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:287)_
- `B34-007`: <code>EntryPoint</code> fields are symbol/file ID, qualified/basename, relative file, optional line, and typed reason (lines 88-97). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:288)_
- `B34-008`: <code>EntryPointReason</code> variants are <code>Orphan</code>, <code>WellKnownName(String)</code>, <code>FrameworkRoute(String)</code>, and <code>Test</code> (lines 99-105). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:289)_
- `B34-009`: <code>detect_entry_points(file_graph, payloads) → Vec&lt;EntryPoint&gt;</code> consumes <code>CodeGraph</code> and <code>SymbolGraphFilePayload</code> slices (lines 108-115). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:290)_
- `B34-010`: Orphan heuristic considers only files with no dependents and includes them only when their payload has at least one outgoing call; emitted ID/name/file are relative path, line is absent, reason is <code>Orphan</code> (lines 118-125). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:291)_
- `B34-011`: Well-known-name heuristic excludes <code>&lt;module&gt;</code>, is case-sensitive, uses per-language names, and emits symbol ID, qualified name, file/line, and the matched name (lines 126-134). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:292)_
- `B34-012`: HTTP framework callees are case-normalized <code>get</code>, <code>post</code>, <code>put</code>, <code>delete</code>, <code>patch</code>, <code>head</code>, <code>options</code>, <code>all</code>, and <code>use</code>, producing <code>framework:http-&lt;name&gt;</code> (lines 135-140). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:293)_
- `B34-013`: Callee <code>route</code> produces exact reason <code>framework:route</code> (lines 139-140). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:294)_
- `B34-014`: Framework hits are attributed to the enclosing symbol found by line-range containment at the call site (lines 141-145). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:295)_
- `B34-015`: Test heuristic excludes <code>&lt;module&gt;</code>, matches <code>^test[_A-Z]</code>, <code>^Test[A-Z_]</code>, or exact <code>it</code>/<code>describe</code>, and emits reason <code>Test</code> (lines 146-151). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:296)_
- `B34-016`: Dedup key is ordered <code>(id, reason)</code>; the same symbol may appear under different reasons but never twice under the same reason (lines 153-155). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:297)_
- `B34-017`: <code>find_symbol_at</code> sorts once by start line, checks inclusive <code>start ≤ target ≤ end</code>, and returns the deepest enclosure (highest start) (lines 157-162). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:298)_
- `B34-018`: Dispatcher tool name is <code>entry_points</code>; it gets project cache/file graph, lazily collects candidates, calls detector, and returns JSON <code>{ entries }</code> (lines 164-177). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:299)_
- `B34-019`: p99 target is under 500 ms on 10K files by loading payloads only for orphans and conventional filenames such as <code>main.*</code>, <code>index.*</code>, <code>__main__.py</code>, and <code>Main.kt</code> (lines 179-185). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:300)_
- `B34-020`: Files that are neither orphan candidates nor conventionally named must not cause payload loads (line 185). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:301)_
- `B34-021`: On <code>TechStackDecision::ExistingProject</code>, <code>research_decompose</code> calls the tool and injects <code>EXISTING_ENTRY_POINTS</code> into Designer context (lines 187-191). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:302)_
- `B34-022`: Designer instruction grounds every Phase in exactly one entry-point group and every Step in a code path traced from one entry point (lines 192-195). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:303)_
- `B34-023`: <code>/research</code> pre-queries the tool and injects <code>PROJECT_STARTING_POINTS</code> during Oracle formulation; tree expansion uses these as seeds (lines 197-201). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:304)_
- `B34-024`: <code>EntryPointsDetected</code> payload is <code>{ project_id, count, by_reason: BTreeMap&lt;String,u32&gt; }</code> and emits once when Designer or Researcher invokes the tool (lines 203-205). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:305)_
- `B34-025`: Produced symbols are detector, enclosure helper, entry types, name table, SSB variant, and dispatcher registration (lines 207-216). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:306)_
- `B34-026`: Consumers are research decomposition, CLI research formulation, and dashboard <code>list_entry_points</code>; reachability includes CLI research, dispatcher calls, and TaskRunner Designer briefing (lines 217-228). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:307)_
- `B34-027`: Empty vector is the defined “no entries” result; detector must not return <code>Result&lt;Vec, Error&gt;</code> (lines 229-232). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:308)_
- `B34-028`: Stub/contract invariants are p99 budget and impossibility of duplicate <code>(id, reason)</code>; B-33 graph/decomposition mode may highlight the results (lines 232-236). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:309)_
- `B34-029`: Observable research fixture expects <code>EntryPointsDetected</code> populated with at least GET, POST, and test reasons and an Oracle answer beginning at real files (lines 237-243). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:310)_
- `B34-030`: CLI deterministic output fixture includes Rust/Go <code>main</code>, C# <code>Main</code>, orphan <code>index.ts</code>, tests, and Express GET routes (lines 245-252). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:311)_
- `B34-031`: Manual performance check targets under 500 ms; Designer and Researcher logs/output must show injected blocks/routes (lines 254-268). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:312)_
- `B34-032`: Payload-selectivity evidence on 10K files requires <code>N &lt;&lt; 1000</code>, concretely under 10% (lines 276-280). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:313)_
- `B34-033`: Acceptance adds a stricter small-repo target under 100 ms, all four heuristics, 14-language table plus Python special case, deepest enclosure, CLI/IPC registration, both injections, event emission, selective loads, dedup, zero production unwraps, and no unconfirmed crates (lines 283-302). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:314)_
- `B34-034`: Orphan tests distinguish depended-on A, outgoing-call B, and inert C (lines 304-310). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:315)_
- `B34-035`: Name tests cover every language and case sensitivity: Rust <code>Main</code> is rejected; C# <code>Main</code> is accepted (lines 310-314). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:316)_
- `B34-036`: Framework tests cover <code>app.get</code>, <code>router.post</code>, and <code>Route::get</code> with correct enclosing attribution (lines 315-318). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:317)_
- `B34-037`: Test-name fixture expects <code>test_foo</code>, <code>Test_bar</code>, <code>it</code>, and <code>describe</code> but not <code>regular_func</code> (lines 319-321). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:318)_
- `B34-038`: Dedup test requires two records for one symbol under Orphan and WellKnownName, neither merged nor duplicated (lines 322-325). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:319)_
- `B34-039`: Performance/selectivity tests use synthetic 10K files and a recording cache; slow-CI timing may be skipped locally, while dispatcher contract freezes JSON shape (lines 326-333). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:320)_
- `B34-040`: File lifecycle creates four entry-point module files and decisions, exports them, registers dispatcher/SSB/UI/CLI, and modifies Designer/Researcher integration (lines 335-358). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:321)_
- `B34-041`: V2 <code>list_entry_points</code> can feed a B-33 graph or, if its alternate placement wins, orbit MAP mode (lines 351-355). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:322)_
- `B34-042`: Project <code>[entry_points]</code> append-only customization is only a decision-doc recommendation, not a settled task; the orientation summary overstates it as an implemented/firm configuration behavior (lines 360-370). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:323)_
- `B34-043`: Potential <code>subscribe</code>/<code>on</code>/<code>register</code> framework patterns are discussion-only; default is parity with the exact HTTP/route set (lines 362-366). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:324)_
- `B34-044`: <code>include_tests: bool</code> is an unresolved decision; recommendation is default include and consumer-side filtering (lines 378-380). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:325)_
- `B34-045`: Side benefits include accurate decomposition/research, B-33 highlighting, B-25 discovery, possible B-23 table growth, and user-facing <code>/entry_points</code> exploration (lines 382-395). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:326)_
- `B34-046`: Mandatory inputs are the full SocratiCode detector/table, B-29 symbol graph, Designer research tree, and Oracle implementation-order source (lines 44-57). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:327)_
- `B34-047`: The source has no SHIPPED header and all acceptance boxes remain open; “PLANNED” comes from the orientation summary rather than this canonical file (lines 1-402). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:328)_
- `B34-048`: B-29 is required; B-33 consumes highlights; B-35 is independent but co-feeds Designer context (lines 397-402). _(anchor: DEEP-B-002.discovery.md:329)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0266`: section=`B-34 - Entry-point detection - PLANNED` Candidate union has four heuristic families: Orphan means a file has no dependents but has outgoing calls; WellKnownName matches `main`, `Main`, or `__main__` with language-specific case rules; FrameworkRoute matches HTTP method callees or `route`; Test matches the specified test-name regexes plus exact `it`/`describe`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:460)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0267`: section=`B-34 - Entry-point detection - PLANNED` Framework reasons are `framework:http-X` or `framework:route`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:461)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0268`: section=`B-34 - Entry-point detection - PLANNED` Deduplicate on `(id, reason)`, allowing the same symbol under two distinct reasons. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:462)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0269`: section=`B-34 - Entry-point detection - PLANNED` Target is p99 below 500ms on a 10K-file project by loading payloads only for orphan and conventional-filename candidates, under 10% of files. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:463)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0270`: section=`B-34 - Entry-point detection - PLANNED` Designer ExistingProject receives `EXISTING_ENTRY_POINTS`; Researcher `/research` receives `PROJECT_STARTING_POINTS`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:464)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0271`: section=`B-34 - Entry-point detection - PLANNED` `[entry_points]` in `elai_cc.toml` appends `ENTRY_POINT_NAMES` to built-ins rather than replacing them. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:465)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0272`: section=`B-34 - Entry-point detection - PLANNED` Cross-cutting event name is `EntryPointsDetected` (lines 981-988). _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:466)_

### B-35: Context-artifacts pipeline
- **Status:** planned
- **Anchors:** `B35-001` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:276; `B35-002` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:277; `B35-003` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:278; `B35-004` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:279; `B35-005` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:280; `B35-006` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:281; `B35-007` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:282; `B35-008` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:283; `B35-009` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:284; `B35-010` @ DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:285
- **Composes with:**
  - `B-002/X-008` ↔ B-31: B-31 explicitly enables B-35 artifact indexing to coexist with code indexing through shared lock primitives (B-31 lines 462-465).
  - `B-004/X-E09` ↔ B-23, B-31, B-40, B-41, B-42: B-42 is structurally B-35 storage/retrieval plus B-23 scheduling plus B-31 locking; B-40 project classification and B-41 audit modes select and consume its briefings (B-42 L13-L22, L57-L76, L219-L242).
  - `B-005/X06` ↔ B-1, B-29, B-31, B-32, B-33, B-34: : B-29 requires B-1’s canonical DKG path, integrates B-31 watcher invalidation, backs B-33 symbol visualization, shares cache payloads with B-34, and is parallel-safe with B-32/B-35 (<code>F06:1-10, 206-229, 301-302, 429-448</code>).
  - `B-005/X07` ↔ B-1, B-31, B-32: : B-32 requires B-1, is a natural B-31 batch-refactor sibling, adds minified telemetry to B-31’s progress panel, and supplies B-35’s artifact chunking (<code>F07:1-10, 205-228, 319-337, 373-379</code>).
  - `B-006/INT-001` ↔ B-16, B-19: : B-16’s blake3-over-blake2 rule directly governs B-19’s phase-4C hash drift and B-35’s SHA-256-to-blake3 port (B16 lines 169-180; B19 lines 52-54; B35 lines 21-27, 49-50).
  - `B-006/INT-007` ↔ B-7: : B-7’s TechStackDecision producer is wire-dark, while B-35 targets TechStackDecision::ExistingProject briefing injection. The artifact briefing can be specified independently, but a common live decision object is an integration prerequisit
  - `B-006/INT-012` ↔ B-23: : B-35 explicitly leaves artifact data as future fuel for B-23 Layer-2 proposals; it is an unlocked side benefit, not part of B-23’s current inputs (B35 lines 577-581, 595-596).
  - `B-006/INT-014` ↔ B-31, B-32: : B-35 depends on B-31’s lock lifecycle and B-32’s line/character chunkers; the exact artifact lock scope remains open (B35 lines 9-13, 181-185, 210-215).
  - `B-006/INT-015`: : B-35 inserts ArtifactStage into the existing QueryPipeline and adds ArtifactCheck to PipelineGrc, connecting knowledge retrieval to a blocking Designer GroundingSweep (B35 lines 44-59, 217-283).
  - `B-006/INT-016` ↔ B-29: : B-29 SymbolGraphCache and B-35 artifacts are complementary Designer context sources; B-35 deliberately keeps artifacts out of the code DKG (B35 lines 9-13, 366-367, 556-558, 588-591).
  - `B-006/INT-017` ↔ B-21, B-23, B-36, B-7: : B-7, B-21, B-23, and B-35 all depend on V2 surfaces or B-36 command wiring: Plan/Research/Orchestration windows, FeasibilityPanel, Proposals/Msi panels, and ArtifactsPanel respectively (B7 lines 111-155; B21 lines 148-160; B23 lines 216-2
  - `B-006/INT-019` ↔ B-16, B-18, B-21, B-23: : B-16’s error/fallback doctrine appears concretely as B-18’s weaker Windows fallback, B-21’s explicit hard kill/escalation, B-23’s reject/pause/revert paths, and B-35’s MalformedGrounding/nonfatal artifact failure behavior.
- **Superseded:** `SUP-059` from `B42-E26`: Produced symbols: `CosmosWorker`, `cosmos_get`, `CosmosStatus::{FRESH,STALE,DECAYED}`, `CosmosStage`, `GroundingMethod::CosmosCheck`, `compile_cosmos_briefing`, four events, and 12 seed artifacts. Consumers are B-35 indexes, ResearchTree, SourceQualityTable, IndexLock, Layer-1A scheduler; reachable from hourly tick, three briefing buil…
- **Superseded:** `SUP-084` from `B35-006`: Hash correction: SocratiCode is described as using SHA-256, but ELAI must use blake3 under the anti-stale rule (lines 21-27, 49-50).
- **Superseded:** `SUP-085` from `B35-017`: Artifact lifecycle states: Status is a closed NotIndexed | Indexed | Stale { changed_files } | Indexing enum; None/Unknown is forbidden (lines 163-168, 357-360).
- **Superseded:** `SUP-086` from `B35-024`: Auto-index lifecycle: After code indexing, IndexingService discovers the manifest, loads states, detects stale files, and reindexes changed files only; update_index repeats staleness detection (lines 202-208, 341-345).
- **Superseded:** `SUP-098` from `SUMMARY/SUM-0375`: Knowledge errors degrade locally: a failed grammar is isolated and recorded; an artifact error cannot block code indexing; a stale Cosmos fetch can warn and use stale data; B-4 embedding failure goes through a circuit-breaker/degradation path rather than panic (B-4, B-30, B-35, B-42).
- `B35-001`: Scope: B-35 is a full second indexing pipeline for project-typed non-code knowledge—schemas, OpenAPI, Kubernetes, ADRs, glossaries, and Terraform—so Designer and Researcher can answer from project conventions rather than inventing them (lines 1-8). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:276)_
- `B35-002`: Dependencies/parallelism: B-1 path unification, B-31 lock primitives, and B-32 chunking are prerequisites; B-29 is a recommended sibling, and B-29/B-30/B-33/B-34 are parallel-safe (lines 9-13). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:277)_
- `B35-003`: Re-scope rationale: The source corrects an earlier “config block” scope to a complete parallel pipeline with its own collection, chunking, embedding/index, staleness, and search contract, auto-indexed with code (lines 15-27). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:278)_
- `B35-004`: Project-state coverage: The pipeline grounds database naming/default/trigger conventions, existing API patterns, deployment files, ADRs, and domain language that code-only grounding misses (lines 29-42). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:279)_
- `B35-005`: Port architecture: The ELAI port has a manifest, per-project context index, reused B-32 chunking, blake3 staleness, ArtifactStage between CatRAG and A-RAG, and real PipelineGrc artifact verification (lines 44-59). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:280)_
- `B35-006`: Hash correction: SocratiCode is described as using SHA-256, but ELAI must use blake3 under the anti-stale rule (lines 21-27, 49-50). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:281)_
- `B35-007`: Manifest format: The source requires a choice between TOML knowledge.artifacts and project-root .elai_artifacts.json, recommends TOML, and later recommends JSON only as a SocratiCode-import fallback; no final choice is recorded (lines 87-98, 540-545). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:282)_
- `B35-008`: Artifact declaration schema: Each declaration has name, file-or-directory path, and nonempty steering description phrased as “check this before X” (lines 99-123, 357-361). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:283)_
- `B35-009`: Example artifact classes: The canonical examples are database-schema, api-spec, ubiquitous-language, and k8s-manifests, with SQL/OpenAPI/domain/deployment descriptions that encode when agents should consult them (lines 99-120). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:284)_
- `B35-010`: Manifest loader: ArtifactManifest::load(project_root) parses declarations and returns Vec<ArtifactSpec> (lines 125-135). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:285)_
- `B35-011`: Name validation: Artifact names must match [a-zA-Z0-9_-]+ because names become collection suffixes (lines 127-129). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:286)_
- `B35-012`: Path security: Relative paths resolve against project_root and are rejected if canonical resolution escapes it, preventing arbitrary system-file reads (lines 130-132). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:287)_
- `B35-013`: Directory expansion: Directory artifacts recursively expand during indexing and obey the project’s existing .elai_ignore rules (lines 133-135). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:288)_
- `B35-014`: ArtifactSpec schema: ArtifactSpec contains name, path, description, and is_dir (lines 137-144). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:289)_
- `B35-015`: ArtifactChunk identity/schema: ArtifactChunk carries a blake3 id of name/path/byte_offset, artifact name/description, relative path, content, start/end lines, whole-file content_hash, and an optional embedding (lines 145-155). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:290)_
- `B35-016`: Index-state schema: ArtifactIndexState contains name, last_indexed_at, deterministic BTreeMap file_hashes, chunk_count, and ArtifactStatus (lines 156-162). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:291)_
- `B35-017`: Artifact lifecycle states: Status is a closed NotIndexed | Indexed | Stale { changed_files } | Indexing enum; None/Unknown is forbidden (lines 163-168, 357-360). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:292)_
- `B35-018`: Incremental index loop: index_artifact reads each expanded file, computes blake3, skips an unchanged hash, and rebuilds only changed files before persisting ArtifactIndexState (lines 171-192). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:293)_
- `B35-019`: Text chunking: Markdown, YAML, JSON, Terraform, and TOML use B-32 chunk_by_lines with CHUNK_SIZE = 50 plus apply_char_cap (lines 181-185). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:294)_
- `B35-020`: SQL chunking: SQL uses simple-regex boundaries at CREATE TABLE, CREATE VIEW, and CREATE INDEX rather than generic line windows (lines 182-184). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:295)_
- `B35-021`: Embedding document shape: Every chunk uses the existing embedding provider and a “context_artifact: {artifact_name}” prefix before content, then upserts into the project context index (lines 185-190). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:296)_
- `B35-022`: Context storage schema: context_chunks has id, artifact_name, relative_path, content, start_line, end_line, embedding, content_hash, and indexed_at; HNSW indexes embeddings and Tantivy indexes content (lines 193-200). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:297)_
- `B35-023`: Project isolation: Artifact storage is named context_{project_id}, kept separate from codebase_/codegraph_ collections and the code DKG (lines 193-200, 492-495, 554-558). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:298)_
- `B35-024`: Auto-index lifecycle: After code indexing, IndexingService discovers the manifest, loads states, detects stale files, and reindexes changed files only; update_index repeats staleness detection (lines 202-208, 341-345). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:299)_
- `B35-025`: Failure containment: Artifact indexing failures warn and continue; they may never compromise or block code-index integrity (lines 206-208, 448-450, 492-495). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:300)_
- `B35-026`: Failure-event shape gap: The missing-path check expects ArtifactsIndexed to contain a failed list, but the declared ArtifactsIndexed payload lists only indexed, up_to_date, total_chunks, and latency_ms (lines 303-306 versus 419-423). failed is required by the check but absent from the schema. _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:301)_
- `B35-027`: Cross-process lock: Artifact indexing either shares B-31 LockScope::Index with code indexing or receives LockScope::Artifacts; shared scope is recommended to avoid embedding-provider rate-limit collision, but the decision is open (lines 210-215, 549-551). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:302)_
- `B35-028`: Retrieval placement: ArtifactStage runs between CatRAG and A-RAG in STANDARD and DEEP tiers; FAST remains code-only (lines 217-227). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:303)_
- `B35-029`: Retrieval algorithm/result: Artifact search fuses semantic embedding and BM25 via RRF and returns ArtifactSearchResult values with score, content, and artifact metadata (lines 219-225). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:304)_
- `B35-030`: Dispatcher search defaults: search_artifacts accepts project_id, query, optional artifact_name, and limit defaulting to 10 (lines 229-240). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:305)_
- `B35-031`: Four dispatcher tools: list_artifacts returns declarations/status, index_artifacts force-reindexes, search_artifacts searches semantically with optional filter, and remove_artifacts drops the context collection (lines 241-247). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:306)_
- `B35-032`: Seventh grounding method: GroundingMethod adds ArtifactCheck { artifact_name, query } after FileCheck, SourceCheck, TestCheck, WebCheck, ApiCheck, and ApiRegistryCheck (lines 249-263). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:307)_
- `B35-033`: Artifact verdicts: ArtifactCheck dispatches filtered search; any score > 0.5 is Verified, no qualifying result is Unverified, and an undeclared name is MalformedGrounding with reason “artifact not declared” (lines 264-268). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:308)_
- `B35-034`: Grounding gate behavior: Designer claims should cite the relevant artifact; GroundingSweep blocks a project-relevant claim lacking artifact backing and any other grounding source (lines 270-276). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:309)_
- `B35-035`: Designer briefing: TechStackDecision::ExistingProject injects AVAILABLE_ARTIFACTS as name/description pairs and tells Designer to search the database/API/infrastructure/domain artifact before decisions (lines 278-283, 350-351). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:310)_
- `B35-036`: Dashboard panel: ArtifactsPanel shows name, path, description, status, chunk count, and last-indexed time; it exposes per-artifact Re-index and filtered Search and shows the first 200 description characters (lines 285-295). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:311)_
- `B35-037`: Canonical Tauri commands: list_project_artifacts, search_project_artifact, reindex_project_artifact, and remove_project_artifacts are the four explicit command signatures (lines 297-301, 337-339). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:312)_
- `B35-038`: Command-name drift: The later file plan instead calls the V2 commands list_artifacts, index_artifacts, search_artifacts, and remove_artifacts to match stub.ts (lines 520-526). The source does not reconcile those names with the project-prefixed signatures. _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:313)_
- `B35-039`: Artifact events: ArtifactsIndexed carries project_id, indexed, up_to_date, total_chunks, and latency_ms; ArtifactSearched carries project_id, optional artifact name, query, result count, and latency_ms; ArtifactStale carries project_id, artifact name, and changed files (lines 303-307). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:314)_
- `B35-040`: Activity surface terminology: The source says ActivityFeed displays the three events (line 308), although its V2 file plan mounts ArtifactsPanel in Workspace/KnowledgeDrawer (lines 527-531); it does not specify the V2 orbit mapping. _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:315)_
- `B35-041`: User documentation: docs/examples/elai_cc.example.toml must show SQL, OpenAPI, Kubernetes, Terraform, ADR, and glossary declarations and explain description steering; dashboard-design may receive a panel mockup only if mutable (lines 310-320). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:316)_
- `B35-042`: Wiring producers: The contract produces manifest/types/index/search/stage symbols, ArtifactCheck, three SSB variants, four dispatcher entries, four dashboard commands, and ArtifactsPanel (lines 322-340). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:317)_
- `B35-043`: Wiring consumers: build_index and update_index invoke artifact indexing/staleness; QueryPipeline consumes ArtifactStage; verify_claim dispatches ArtifactCheck; run_designer_briefing injects AVAILABLE_ARTIFACTS (lines 341-351). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:318)_
- `B35-044`: Reachability: CLI index, CLI research, dashboard submit, and IndexingService build paths must reach the pipeline (lines 352-356). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:319)_
- `B35-045`: Stub bans: Status cannot be unknown, descriptions cannot be empty, context_{project_id} must exist after indexing, and ArtifactCheck must validate declaration before search rather than silently return ungrounded (lines 357-365). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:320)_
- `B35-046`: Observable workflow: Declaring a real SQL artifact then indexing must produce ArtifactsIndexed with total_chunks > 0; a filtered “users” search must return the CREATE TABLE users chunk (lines 368-375). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:321)_
- `B35-047`: Staleness recovery check: Editing schema.sql then indexing should emit ArtifactStale and reindex only database-schema (lines 391-395). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:322)_
- `B35-048`: Removal lifecycle: remove_artifacts empties or drops context_chunks (lines 414-417). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:323)_
- `B35-049`: Missing-path recovery: A nonexistent artifact path must warn, record failure without panic, and preserve the code index (lines 419-423). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:324)_
- `B35-050`: Escape rejection evidence: A ../../etc/passwd declaration must fail manifest load with an outside-project-root error (lines 425-428). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:325)_
- `B35-051`: Session-path drift: The grounding SQL check uses .elai_cc/.elai/sessions/ssb.db (lines 401-407), which is not reconciled in this source with B-1’s .elai_cc path unification prerequisite. _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:326)_
- `B35-052`: Acceptance gates: Completion requires manifest parsing/validation, B-32 and SQL chunking, blake3 staleness, project context storage, STANDARD/DEEP stage routing, four tools, four commands, three events, ArtifactCheck and Designer use, transparent reindex, nonfatal errors, secure paths, collision-free locking, an example manifest, zero production unwrap, and no unapproved dependency (lines 431-457). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:327)_
- `B35-053`: Manifest tests: Tests parse 5+ artifacts, reject escape/invalid names, and support file and directory paths (lines 459-465). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:328)_
- `B35-054`: Chunk/index tests: SQL boundaries, YAML/JSON/Markdown windows, and populated artifact_name/description fields are tested (lines 466-470). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:329)_
- `B35-055`: Idempotent staleness tests: First index builds all, unchanged reindex skips all, one modification rebuilds one, a new directory file adds one, and a removed file deletes its chunks (lines 471-476). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:330)_
- `B35-056`: Search tests: Search works across all artifacts or one name and returns score, content, and metadata (lines 477-480). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:331)_
- `B35-057`: Grounding tests: Above-threshold results verify, undeclared names are malformed, and no above-threshold result is unverified (lines 481-484). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:332)_
- `B35-058`: Briefing/UI tests: ExistingProject injects AVAILABLE_ARTIFACTS while Greenfield skips it; V2 E2E renders status and exercises live Re-index updates (lines 485-491). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:333)_
- `B35-059`: Contract tests: Artifact failures must not block code indexing, and context collections must remain isolated from code collections (lines 492-495). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:334)_
- `B35-060`: File lifecycle: The source enumerates six artifact modules, ArtifactStage, pipeline/export/indexing/dispatcher/task-runner/grounding/CLI/dashboard/SSB changes, example/docs, and a decision record (lines 497-536). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:335)_
- `B35-061`: Threshold/config choice: The decision record must choose fixed ArtifactCheck score >0.5 versus per-artifact override; default 0.5 is recommended (lines 554-555). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:336)_
- `B35-062`: Explicit graph non-goal: Artifacts should not produce DKG nodes; the recommendation is to keep DKG code-only and use artifact search (lines 556-558). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:337)_
- `B35-063`: Checkpoint granularity: Artifact state is recommended per artifact, not per B-31 batch, because artifacts are typically under 100 files and whole-artifact rebuild is acceptable (lines 559-561). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:338)_
- `B35-064`: Integration outcomes: B-35 provides non-code ExistingProject context, grounded research, DDD vocabulary control, an Empire-spec path, and possible B-23 proposal fuel (lines 566-583). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:339)_
- `B35-065`: Compliance boundary: SOC2/HIPAA/GDPR documents may be declared as artifacts to ground future compliance-aware plans, but the source presents this as a foundation/side benefit, not a shipped compliance guarantee (lines 584-586). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:340)_
- `B35-066`: Lifecycle: B-29, B-31, B-34, and B-23 are the named downstream/complementary relationships (lines 588-596). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:341)_
- `B35-067`: Collection-name tension: Artifact name is validated because it is said to become a collection suffix (lines 127-129), while storage is specified as one context_{project_id} collection with artifact_name rows (lines 193-200). The source does not choose per-artifact collections versus one per-project collection. _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:342)_
- `B35-068`: Status: B-35 has no shipped overlay and every acceptance box is unchecked; it is a planned pipeline (lines 431-457). _(anchor: DEEP-B-006.discovery.md:343)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0273`: section=`B-35 - Context-artifacts pipeline - PLANNED` `[[knowledge.artifacts]]` declarations contain `name`, file-or-directory `path`, and a description phrased as `check this before X`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:472)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0274`: section=`B-35 - Context-artifacts pipeline - PLANNED` Each project gets a separate `context_*` vector-index collection, independent of the code DKG. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:473)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0275`: section=`B-35 - Context-artifacts pipeline - PLANNED` Non-code text reuses B-32 chunking; SQL chunks at `CREATE TABLE`, `VIEW`, or `INDEX` regex boundaries. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:474)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0276`: section=`B-35 - Context-artifacts pipeline - PLANNED` Staleness uses blake3; `ProjectMetadataStore` is idempotent. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:475)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0277`: section=`B-35 - Context-artifacts pipeline - PLANNED` `ArtifactStage` participates in STANDARD and DEEP; FAST remains code only. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:476)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0278`: section=`B-35 - Context-artifacts pipeline - PLANNED` `GroundingMethod::ArtifactCheck` is the seventh verification method. It dispatches `search_artifacts` with an artifact-name filter, returns Verified if a result score exceeds 0.5, and returns `MalformedGrounding` when the artifact was not declared. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:477)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0279`: section=`B-35 - Context-artifacts pipeline - PLANNED` Dispatcher tools: `list_artifacts`, `index_artifacts`, `search_artifacts`, `remove_artifacts` (the summary abbreviates these as list/index/search/remove_artifacts). _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:478)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0280`: section=`B-35 - Context-artifacts pipeline - PLANNED` Events: `ArtifactsIndexed`, `ArtifactSearched`, `ArtifactStale`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:479)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0281`: section=`B-35 - Context-artifacts pipeline - PLANNED` Designer ExistingProject automatically receives `AVAILABLE_ARTIFACTS`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:480)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0282`: section=`B-35 - Context-artifacts pipeline - PLANNED` Manifest loading rejects paths resolving outside `project_root`. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:481)_
- `SUMMARY/SUM-0283`: section=`B-35 - Context-artifacts pipeline - PLANNED` Artifact-pipeline errors are non-fatal and never block code indexing. _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:482)_

### B-36: Dashboard V2 Track D wiring (13 commands + 15 surfaces)
- **Status:** shipped — open cancel/orphan defects remain

- **Composes with:**

  - `B-002/X-012` ↔ B-12, B-2: B-2’s phase events become visible only after both producer wiring and a live V2 read path; B-12 establishes where the StatusBar lives, while B-36 (outside this chunk) remains the named backend bridge.
  - `B-002/X-015` ↔ B-12, B-2, B-24, B-31, B-34, B-4: V2 commands are the common bridge for B-2 state, B-4 knowledge, B-24 media, B-31 cancellation/progress, and B-34 entry-point display; exact command registration is repeatedly delegated to B-36 rather than owned by B-12’s shell.
  - `B-004/X-E01` ↔ B-5, B-8: B-5 defines producers/surfaces but explicitly relies on B-8 for production firing; B-36 later names live `get_grc_budgets` as a V2-only handler “post-B-5” (B-5 L1-L9, L173-L176; B-36 L196-L212).

  - `B-004/X-E07` ↔ B-29, B-31, B-33, B-7: B-33 backend availability depends on B-36 Session-2 AppState/engine dependencies, while its data depends on B-7 DecompositionTree and B-29 SymbolGraphCache; Refresh relies on B-31 watcher behavior (B-33 L9-L14, L298-L305, L335-L344).
  - `B-004/X-E08` ↔ B-10, B-4, B-5, B-6: B-36’s real V2 handlers explicitly consume B-4 `get_search_mode`, B-5 `get_grc_budgets`, B-6 `get_evidence_gates`, and B-10 `get_context_budget`, making B-36 their dashboard adapter rather than their producer (B-36 L196-L212).
  - `B-004/X-I01` ↔ B-14: A unified V2 chat path likely uses B-36 AppState/subprocess/event plumbing to host B-14’s `stream_chat` and cancellation handle. Both halves are explicit, but neither source states the final shared type/module boundary.
  - `B-004/X-I02` ↔ B-5: Exact live frontend naming should converge on `get_grc_budgets`: B-5 says stub names remain 1:1 and its contract produces that identifier; B-36 consumes exactly it. The longer B-5 prose name is likely a descriptive predecessor, but this rem
  - `B-004/X-I07` ↔ B-33: If B-33 chooses orbit MAP (Option B), the work becomes an explicit exception to B-36’s “do not touch orbit unless asked” constraint and therefore requires the user choice documented by B-33.
  - `B-004/X-I08` ↔ B-14: B-14’s “no thinking messages” and B-36’s live `web_research_step` imply progress should flow through Activity/orbit while Command displays milestones/tokens/interventions; this composed routing is consistent but not specified as one end-to-
  - `B-004/X-I09` ↔ B-27: B-27’s pre-call redaction and spend gate should wrap any cloud-backed execution reached from dashboard commands, but B-36’s D.7 subprocess environment does not explicitly call `CloudModeRouter` or `RedactingBabyStepExecutor`.

- `B36-E01`: Implement 13 Track-D command rows plus 15 V2-only surfaces in `dashboard-v2/src-tauri/src/lib.rs`, replace matching `stub.ts` functions with live invokes, and keep B-29..B-35 parallel-safe; prerequisites are B-1/B-2/B-3/B-7. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:286)_

  - `E-B37-001` → B-1, B-15, B-18, B-20, B-22, B-23, B-24, B-25, B-27, B-28: Goal, prerequisites, and concurrency. `[E]` Create one `RuntimeAdapter` for 11 backends, per-role hybrid routing, a backend fallback chain separate from model fallback, and Tier-C isolation for every 
  - `E-B37-028` → B-38: Follow-on. `[E]` Next is B-38 or the next outstanding B-N depending on priority. (B-37:L251-L254)
- `E-B37-001`: Goal, prerequisites, and concurrency. `[E]` Create one `RuntimeAdapter` for 11 backends, per-role hybrid routing, a backend fallback chain separate from model fallback, and Tier-C isolation for every CLI subprocess. Estimate 3-4 sessions; prerequisites B-1, B-15..B-20, B-22, B-18, and completed Phase 3J variants; parallel-safe with B-23/B-24/B-25/B-27/B-28. (B-37:L1-L12) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:318)_
- `E-B37-002`: Runtime architecture. `[E]` Orchestrator emits Anthropic-shaped messages once; adapters translate protocols, enabling combinations such as cloud Designer, local Executor, and Claude-Code Auditor without orchestrator forks. (B-37:L14-L33) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:319)_
- `E-B37-003`: Exact backend set. `[E]` Existing: `Sidecar`, `AnthropicApi`; CLI ports: `ClaudeCode`, `CodexCli`, `OpenCode`, `Hermes`, `GeminiCli`, `KiroCli`, `CopilotCli`; native HTTP: `Ollama`, `Vllm`. (B-37:L35-L39) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:320)_
- `E-B37-004`: Process boundary/security. `[E]` All seven CLI adapters run inside Phase-4A Tier C; the CLI binary is the agent boundary and existing IPC sanitization prevents raw stdout from entering prompt context. (B-37:L41-L44) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:321)_
- `E-B37-005`: V2 control surface. `[E]` Add RuntimePanel to V2 Settings plus Tauri `get_runtime_status`, `set_role_runtime`, and `test_runtime_health`. (B-37:L46-L52) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:322)_
- `E-B37-006`: Third-party provenance artifact. `[E]` Run source-mode paper ingestion over specified Ouroboros files and produce `workflow/papers/ouroboros-runtime-interface.md` tagged MEDIUM because it is reviewed third-party MIT code. (B-37:L63-L68) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:323)_
- `E-B37-007`: Core types. `[E]` Add the 11-variant `RuntimeBackend` and `RuntimeAdapter`; alias to `LlmProvider` only if vtable-compatible, otherwise use a thin wrapper. (B-37:L70-L72) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:324)_
- `E-B37-008`: Resolver behavior. `[E]` `resolve_backend(candidate)` returns a typed `ResolveBackendError` containing the complete supported list on an unknown name. (B-37:L74-L76) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:325)_
- `E-B37-009`: Runtime factory lifecycle. `[E]` `create_runtime(RuntimeFactoryConfig) -> Box<dyn RuntimeAdapter>` initially ports Sidecar/Anthropic and returns stub errors for new backends until their adapters land. (B-37:L78-L80) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:326)_
- `E-B37-010`: One-release configuration migration. `[E]` Migrate `[backend]` to `[runtime]`; alias `mode="local"` -> `backend="sidecar"` and `mode="api"` -> `backend="anthropic_api"`, emitting a deprecation warning for one release. (B-37:L82-L85) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:327)_
- `E-B37-011`: CLI adapter protocol. `[E]` Each CLI adapter uses subprocess async I/O, translates Anthropic messages to CLI stdin, parses CLI stdout back to Anthropic responses, and applies `permission_mode` where supported. (B-37:L87-L92) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:328)_
- `E-B37-012`: CLI adapter failure/timeouts. `[E]` Enforce `startup_output_timeout_seconds` and `stdout_idle_timeout_seconds`; missing CLI makes `health_check()` return `Unavailable`, never panic; sanitation and Tier C remain mandatory. (B-37:L92-L97) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:329)_
- `E-B37-013`: Native HTTP adapters. `[E]` Ollama uses `/api/chat`; vLLM uses `/v1/chat/completions`; both existing 3J providers move to the common factory signature. (B-37:L98-L100) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:330)_
- `E-B37-014`: Per-role configuration/defaults. `[E]` `[agents.<role>.runtime] backend="..."`; default AnthropicApi for designer/auditor/researcher/media_validator and Sidecar for executor/memory_writer/context_compiler/prompt_engineer. (B-37:L102-L110) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:331)_
- `E-B37-015`: Runtime fallback state machine. `[E]` On `Unavailable`, traverse `fallback_chain`, emit `runtime_fallback` with the traversed chain, then ESCALATE in INTERACTIVE or HALT in AUTONOMOUS when exhausted. This is orthogonal to 4G `SUBSTITUTE_MODEL`. (B-37:L112-L116) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:332)_
- `E-B37-016`: CLI runtime control. `[E]` `/runtime show` prints roles/backends; `/runtime set <role> <backend>` overrides; `/runtime fallback <role> <b1>,<b2>` sets the chain. (B-37:L118-L121) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:333)_
- `E-B37-017`: RuntimePanel behavior. `[E]` Show per-role backend and chain, green/yellow/red health for known backends, and a canary “Test backend” action. (B-37:L123-L127) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:334)_
- `E-B37-018`: Metrics schema. `[E]` `.elai_cc/metrics/phase_4M.toml` records per-backend p50/p99 latency, tokens/sec, cost per million tokens or per hour, fallback fire rate, and health-check uptime percentage. (B-37:L129-L131) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:335)_
- `E-B37-019`: Migration guide. `[E]` `workflow/papers/phase-4M-migration.md` must give a worked old `[backend] mode` -> new `[runtime] backend` example. (B-37:L133-L134) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:336)_
- `E-B37-020`: Exact symbol/event contract. `[E]` Core types also include `RuntimeFactoryConfig`, `PermissionMode`, `PerRoleRuntime`, `RuntimeCapabilities`, and `RuntimeHealth`; nine new adapter modules are named; SSB actions are `runtime_fallback`, `runtime_health_changed`, `cli_subprocess_spawned`, and `cli_subprocess_killed`. (B-37:L136-L150) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:337)_
- `E-B37-021`: Consumers/reachability. `[E]` Reuse existing providers, Tier C, and 4G lifecycle; configuration, Settings RuntimePanel, and CLI `/runtime` are all entry surfaces. (B-37:L151-L159) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:338)_
- `E-B37-022`: Permission/security invariants. `[E]` No CLI adapter may bypass Tier C; `PermissionMode` defaults to `AcceptEdits`; `DangerouslySkipPermissions` is explicit opt-in only; stdout must be sanitized before prompt context. (B-37:L160-L167) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:339)_

- `E-B37-024`: Structural checks. `[E]` Verify all 11 enum variants, the trait, resolver/factory, at least 11 adapter test groups plus integration, `SlashCommand::Runtime`, Tier-C references, and the migration guide. (B-37:L175-L199) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:341)_
- `E-B37-025`: Acceptance/done gates. `[E]` Acceptance covers ingestion, all adapters, no 3J regression, graceful missing CLIs, Tier-C contract evidence, sanitizer, permission default, idle kill, native migration, role config, orthogonal fallback, CLI/UI, metrics, guide, and tests; every box is unchecked. ### B-38: Continuous ambiguity gate
- **Status:** shipped — index-inferred from schema/enums (no standalone memory block)
- **Anchors:** `B38-001` @ DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:218; `B38-002` @ DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:219; `B38-003` @ DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:220; `B38-004` @ DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:221; `B38-005` @ DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:222; `B38-006` @ DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:223; `B38-007` @ DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:224; `B38-008` @ DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:225; `B38-009` @ DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:226; `B38-010` @ DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:227
- **Composes with:**
  - `B-001/X-003` ↔ B-10, B-11, B-20, B-41: B-11’s visual rules govern all B-10/B-20/B-38/B-41 panels: exact type/color/layout tokens and no decorative animation remain global even for newly added Gates, failure, context, and audit surfaces. (B11:L72-138)
  - `B-001/X-006` ↔ B-17, B-22, B-41, B-8: B-38 gates PLANNING -> TESTING_FIRST using existing B-8/B-17 grounding; B-41’s per-tier gates occur after decomposition outputs and feed B-22 auto-advance. Ambiguity readiness does not substitute for zero-BLOCK tier evidence. (B38:L132-177;
- **Superseded:** `SUP-003` from `B0-028`: B-38 is explicitly SHIPPED, not PLANNED: three ambiguity-gate SSB types and schema 42 -> 43; status is inferred by the index from schema comments and enum variants because there is no standalone memory block. (B0:L166-170)
- `B38-001`: Continuous ambiguity is `1 - Σ(clarity_i * weight_i)` and qualifies overall at `<=0.2`; it augments, rather than replaces, existing `ground_plan` UNGROUNDED checks. (B38:L1-18) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:218)_
- `B38-002`: Motivation: binary grounding cannot distinguish one thin citation from thorough grounding, should exempt intentional “decide later” deferrals, and is vulnerable to one noisy snapshot. (B38:L20-27) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:219)_
- `B38-003`: Greenfield weights are Goal 0.40, Constraint 0.30, Success 0.30. Brownfield weights are Goal 0.35, Constraint 0.25, Success 0.25, Context 0.15. (B38:L29-46, L91-108) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:220)_
- `B38-004`: Floors are Goal 0.75, Constraint 0.65, Success 0.70, and brownfield Context 0.60; any floor can block despite aggregate readiness. (B38:L41-46, L91-108) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:221)_
- `B38-005`: Reproducibility uses `SCORING_TEMPERATURE=0.1`; readiness requires `AUTO_COMPLETE_STREAK_REQUIRED=2` consecutive qualifying snapshots. (B38:L45-49, L91-108) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:222)_
- `B38-006`: Concept mapping is Goal -> spec-verifiable plan goal; Constraint -> CLAUDE hard rules/project boundaries; Success -> measurable ACCEPTANCE/DETERMINISTIC blocks; Context -> DKG/RepoMap/CLAUDE auxiliary context. (B38:L34-40) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:223)_
- `B38-007`: Required provenance artifact is `workflow/papers/ouroboros-ambiguity-interface.md` with reliability MEDIUM, created by a source-mode ingestor over Ouroboros’s MIT `ambiguity.py`. (B38:L51-62) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:224)_
- `B38-008`: `ComponentScore` contains `name`, bounded `clarity_score`, bounded `weight`, and `justification`; `ScoreBreakdown` holds goal, constraint, success, plus optional brownfield context. (B38:L64-77) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:225)_
- `B38-009`: `AmbiguityScore` holds `overall_score` and breakdown. Methods are `is_ready()`, `floor_failures(brownfield)`, and `qualifies_for_advance(brownfield)`; qualification is the conjunction of overall readiness and an empty floor-failure list. (B38:L78-88) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:226)_
- `B38-010`: `AmbiguityScorer::score(plan, grounding_report, brownfield, deferrals)` returns `Result<AmbiguityScore,GrcError>` and carries an LLM adapter/model/temperature. Deferrals are explicit input and do not penalize. (B38:L110-121) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:227)_
- `B38-011`: Token budget starts at 2,048 and doubles on truncation for at most five retries; format errors independently allow at most five retries. (B38:L123-125) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:228)_
- `B38-012`: JSON handling accepts raw JSON or Markdown code-block wrapping, clamps scores to `[0.0,1.0]`, substitutes a fallback justification when missing, but treats a missing score as an error. (B38:L126-130) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:229)_
- `B38-013`: Exact gate order: any UNGROUNDED -> BLOCK; else overall >0.2 -> BLOCK with breakdown; else any floor failure -> BLOCK with list; else qualifying streak <2 -> WAIT; else ALLOW. (B38:L132-144) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:230)_
- `B38-014`: Streak is per session in `SessionState` and resets on every plan revision; it must never carry across revisions. (B38:L140-145, L171-176) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:231)_
- `B38-015`: Dashboard milestone labels are `INITIAL` for >0.4, `PROGRESS` for >0.3, `REFINED` for >0.2, and `READY` for <=0.2; GatesPanel also shows dimension bars, floor flags, and `1/2` or `2/2`. (B38:L146-153) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:232)_
- `B38-016`: Events are `ambiguity_scored`, `ambiguity_floor_failed`, and `ambiguity_streak_advanced`, surfaced in OrchestrationDrawer. (B38:L154-155) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:233)_
- `B38-017`: Produced symbols additionally include the scorer and all three score types/methods plus a GatesPanel section; consumers are GroundingSweepGate, PipelineGrc `ground_plan`, and the existing LLM provider. Reachability is PLANNING -> TESTING_FIRST. (B38:L157-177) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:234)_
- `B38-018`: Observable test submits a deliberately vague goal, expects `ambiguity_scored.overall_score > 0.2`, and requires milestone, per-dimension, and floor visuals. (B38:L178-182) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:235)_
- `B38-019`: Acceptance requires at least twelve ambiguity tests, covering greenfield/brownfield, floors, deferral non-penalty, streak, and JSON edge cases, plus exact constants, scoring retries, dashboard, and three live events. (B38:L184-221) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:236)_
- `B38-020`: Intended files are reasoning `ambiguity.rs`/exports, orchestrator gate+SSB, GatesPanel, Tauri `get_ambiguity_state`, provenance artifact, and decision doc. (B38:L223-233) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:237)_
- `B38-021`: The standalone checklist is unchecked, but the later B-0 index says B-38 SHIPPED with events and schema 42 -> 43. This chunk does not prove the paper artifact, dashboard, scorer edge cases, or >=12 tests individually; retain them as specified acceptance, not automatically verified implementation facts. (B38:L203-233; B0:L166-170) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:238)_
- `B38-022`: The acceptance text says “all 11 weight + floor constants,” although the displayed block contains 15 named constants total: threshold, streak, four floors, temperature, three greenfield weights, and four brownfield weights. The intended phrase likely counts a subset, but must not be used as an exact total without clarification. (B38:L91-108, L203-221) _(anchor: DEEP-B-001.discovery.md:239)_

- `E-B40-001`: Goal and blocking contract. `[E]` Insert FRAMING before recursive decomposition, classify the project, run domain-specific parallel research and a 1-3-round ten-category critic, and block decomposition until framing/grounding is complete and the user answers the surfaced tradeoff card. (B-40:L1-L9) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:360)_
- `E-B40-002`: Dependencies, schema, and concurrency. `[E]` Prerequisites B-1/B-22/B-35; source baseline says SSB schema 28 and plans a 28 -> 29 bump for five actions; parallel-safe with B-24/B-25/B-27/B-28/B-37/B-38. (B-40:L10-L16) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:361)_
- `E-B40-003`: Stage/evidence placement. `[E]` FRAMING sits before Idea -> Concepts and adds a seventh AutoAdvance check: at least one round and zero unresolved `C-*`; presets can be refreshed through Phase-4H Layer 1A. (B-40:L18-L33) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:362)_
- `E-B40-004`: Failure prevented. `[E]` Catch wrong-problem decomposition before its asserted 5×5×3×3/225-babystep fanout; B-41 would otherwise detect framing errors late. (B-40:L39-L61) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:363)_

(B-40:L63-L73) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:364)_
- `E-B40-006`: Project classes/classifier. `[E]` `ProjectClass` variants are `ConsumerMobileApp`, `WebApp`, `SaaSDashboard`, `DesktopApp`, `GameMod`, `ResearchTool`, `LibraryOrSDK`, `CLITool`, `Other`; deterministic regex runs first and an LLM only for `Other`. (B-40:L89-L97) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:365)_
- `E-B40-007`: Research-preset schema. `[E]` `[research_presets.<class>]` is an ordered list of streams with `name`, Tera `query_template` using `{idea}`/`{class}`, `min_source_tier` T1/T2, `parallelism_budget`, `cosmos_grounding`, and `mandatory`. Cosmos injection is a B-42 no-op when unavailable; optional streams can be dropped when budget is tight. (B-40:L99-L107) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:366)_
- `E-B40-008`: Default preset floor. `[E]` Seed at least ConsumerMobileApp/WebApp/SaaSDashboard/GameMod; decision docs enumerate each class's mandatory streams. (B-40:L109-L111) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:367)_
- `E-B40-009`: Multi-root research convergence. `[E]` `ResearchTree::new_multi_root(streams)` gives each stream a separate cache slot; every mandatory stream must converge or be named in `degraded_streams`. (B-40:L113-L116) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:368)_
- `E-B40-010`: Framing roles/GRC sequence. `[E]` Add `idea_framer` and `idea_critic` roles, both extending Designer with 5/800 and `[Observe,Hypothesize,Verify,Converge]`. (B-40:L118-L122) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:369)_
- `E-B40-011`: `FramedIdea` schema. `[E]` Fields are `project_class`, `research_summary`, `key_decisions`, `known_unknowns`, and `tradeoff_card`. (B-40:L124-L126) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:370)_
- `E-B40-012`: `TradeoffCard` schema/user workflow. `[E]` Ordered `Tradeoff` entries contain `name`, `options[]`, `default`, and `user_facing_question`; the card surfaces in dashboard/CommandWindow before decomposition. (B-40:L127-L129) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:371)_
- `E-B40-013`: Critic schema. `[E]` `CriticFinding` holds category, `BLOCK/WARN/INFO` severity, a research-stream `evidence_ref`, and `proposed_fix`; categories are STALE, SLOP, POLICY, FEASIBILITY, MARKET, SCOPE, CONTRADICTION, DESIGN_HARD, VAGUE, MISSING. (B-40:L130-L134) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:372)_
- `E-B40-014`: Critic-loop state machine. `[E]` Each round spawns `idea_critic`, groups findings, exits on zero BLOCKs, otherwise feeds `rescope_from_critic` to the framer before the cap; final remaining BLOCK emits `idea_framing_block` and prevents advance. (B-40:L136-L145) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:373)_
- `E-B40-015`: FSM routing and tool permissions. `[E]` Nontrivial `/task` flows PLANNING -> FRAMING -> PLANNING -> FEASIBILITY_PROBE; while FRAMING, PreToolUse permits only research/read and forbids Edit/Write/Bash outside research scratch. (B-40:L147-L151) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:374)_
- `E-B40-016`: Framing evidence derivation. `[E]` `derive_framing_evidence` queries `idea_framed`, `tradeoff_card_emitted`, and absence of unresolved `idea_framing_block`, adding a seventh AutoAdvance check only when state is FRAMING. (B-40:L153-L159) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:375)_
- `E-B40-017`: V2 framing window. `[E]` `IdeaFramingWindow.svelte` is a pre-Workspace first-task screen; it renders the framed idea/card, captures a radio answer for each tradeoff, and submits FRAMING -> PLANNING. (B-40:L161-L165) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:376)_
- `E-B40-018`: Live framing state/UI badge. `[E]` `get_idea_framing_state` must move from stub to live Tauri command; Monitor shows `Critic rounds: N/3` only in FRAMING. (B-40:L166-L169) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:377)_
- `E-B40-019`: Artifact lifecycle. `[E]` Persist `framed-idea.toml`, `critic-rounds/round-N.toml`, and `tradeoff-responses.toml` under `workflow/sessions/{sid}/idea-framing/`; Designer Concepts consumes them through B-35 auto-injection as ground-by-default artifacts. (B-40:L171-L175) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:378)_
- `E-B40-020`: Exact produced-symbol/event contract. `[E]` Produce `FramedIdea`, `TradeoffCard`, ten-variant `CriticCategory`, `run_critic_loop`, `ProjectClass`, `classify_project_class`, `ResearchTree::new_multi_root`, `FsmSubstate::Framing`, two role configs, and actions `idea_framing_started`, `project_class_classified`, `critic_round_complete`, `idea_framed`, `tradeoff_card_emitted`. (B-40:L177-L190) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:379)_
- `E-B40-021`: Consumers and entry reachability. `[E]` Consume ResearchTree, AgentPool spawn, BasicGrcEngine, and SourceQualityTable; reachable from CLI turn on nontrivial `/task` and dashboard framing submission. (B-40:L191-L200) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:380)_
- `E-B40-022`: Stub/budget/research bans. `[E]` Framing state must be live SSB, not mock; framer budget cannot exceed 5/800; mandatory streams cannot be silently skipped and instead become degraded. (B-40:L201-L205) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:381)_
- `E-B40-023`: Observable sequence. `[E]` Example “make an app like PictureThis” classifies ConsumerMobileApp, dispatches six mandatory streams, emits framing started -> classification -> research progress -> 1-3 critic completions -> framed -> card within 5-15 minutes on local 7B. (B-40:L209-L216) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:382)_

- `E-B40-025`: Schema/action checks. `[E]` Deterministic check expects `SCHEMA_VERSION: u32 = 29` and all five new action variants. (B-40:L222-L231) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:384)_
- `E-B40-026`: Type/classifier/category checks. `[E]` Verify `ProjectClass`, `classify_project_class`, and exactly ten uppercase critic variants. (B-40:L233-L239) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:385)_
- `E-B40-027`: Preset/test floor. `[E]` Config must contain at least four presets; run at least 28 framing tests, broken down as 15 unit, eight convergence, and five classifier tests. (B-40:L241-L246) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:386)_
- `E-B40-028`: FSM/evidence/UI/build checks. `[E]` Verify Framing wiring, `derive_framing_evidence`, IdeaFramingWindow existence, clean workspace `cargo check`, and clean `svelte-check`. (B-40:L248-L260) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:387)_
- `E-B40-029`: Classifier acceptance. `[E]` Achieve at least 90% accuracy on 50 labeled prompts committed at `.elai_cc/tests/fixtures/project_class_50.toml`. (B-40:L262-L265) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:388)_
- `E-B40-030`: Research/critic acceptance. `[E]` Ship at least four presets with at least four mandatory streams each; converge within three rounds on 95% of fixture ideas, with remaining 5% correctly emitting `idea_framing_block`. (B-40:L266-L268) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:389)_
- `E-B40-031`: Artifact/gate/UI acceptance. `[E]` Populate all framed-artifact fields; keep existing six autonomous checks passing while adding the seventh; use a live V2 window with no mock fallback. (B-40:L269-L275) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:390)_
- `E-B40-032`: Ablation/build acceptance. `[E]` On 50 ideas, post-framing Concept audit BLOCK rate should fall at least 30%; at least 28 tests and both build checks must pass. All B-40 acceptance boxes are unchecked. (B-40:L276-L278) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:391)_
- `E-B40-033`: Test suite. `[E]` Add 50-fixture classifier/preset/multi-root unit tests, critic convergence/artifact/tradeoff/event contract, final-round block contract, degraded-tier preset test, and at least four additional framing cases on the existing 14-test autonomous gate contract. - `E-B40-035`: FSM design constraint. `[E]` Decisions must justify FRAMING as a PLANNING substate to reuse tool gating and avoid top-level matrix growth. (B-40:L317-L322) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:394)_
- `E-B40-036`: Classifier latency constraint. `[E]` Regex-first is intended to keep the common 90% classification path below 10 ms. (B-40:L323-L324) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:395)_
- `E-B40-037`: Critic taxonomy/cap rationale. `[E]` Decisions must justify exactly ten categories and three rounds; the source attributes the cap to diminishing returns after round two plus one safety-margin round. (B-40:L325-L330) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:396)_
- `E-B40-038`: Honest degradation constraint. `[E]` Missing mandatory research can only be declared degraded, never silently omitted, because downstream concepts would otherwise appear grounded. (B-40:L331-L333) _(anchor: DEEP-B-003.discovery.md:397)_

### B-42: Cosmos worker, briefing compiler, and drawer
- **Status:** shipped — spawn_loop dispatcher deferred
- **Anchors:** `B42-E01` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:338; `B42-E02` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:339; `B42-E03` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:340; `B42-E04` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:341; `B42-E05` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:342; `B42-E06` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:343; `B42-E07` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:344; `B42-E08` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:345; `B42-E09` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:346; `B42-E10` @ DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:347
- **Composes with:**
  - `B-001/X-008` ↔ B-40, B-41: B-41 Concepts consumes B-40 `FramedIdea`; absence degrades to WARN/coarse rules. B-42 is planned grounding fuel for Concepts/Phases, yet its scheduler is deferred, so the audit must tolerate missing fresh Cosmos rather than invent it. (B41:
  - `B-004/X-E09` ↔ B-23, B-31, B-35, B-40, B-41: B-42 is structurally B-35 storage/retrieval plus B-23 scheduling plus B-31 locking; B-40 project classification and B-41 audit modes select and consume its briefings (B-42 L13-L22, L57-L76, L219-L242).
  - `B-004/X-E10` ↔ B-31.5: B-42 intentionally shares `LockScope::Index` with code indexing. Its dedicated contention test is a Cosmos-worker + code-indexer test, distinct from B-31.5’s two-code-indexer contention benchmark (B-42 L143-L156, L342-L349; B-31.5 L175-L223
  - `B-004/X-E12`: B-42’s global `~/.elai/cosmos/` path is intentionally outside project repositories, unlike project-local `.elai_cc` data used by indexing and other B plans; the source explicitly rejects project-local and `/etc` placement (B-42 L57-L63, L38
  - `B-004/X-I04` ↔ B-25, B-5: GRC can eventually present both Empire and Cosmos evidence: B-25 adds `EmpireEventCheck`, B-42 adds `CosmosCheck`, and B-5 exposes reasoning/grounding outcomes. No chunk source promises a dedicated per-method breakdown in ReasoningDrawer.
  - `B-004/X-I05` ↔ B-25: Schema updates must be applied against the repository’s then-current registry rather than literal plan baselines, because B-25 names 101->102 while B-42 says “current +1.” Atomic variant lockstep is explicit only in B-25; applying equivalen
  - `B-004/X-I06` ↔ B-31.5: B-31.5’s durability benchmark does not prove B-42’s same-lock Cosmos/code coexistence unless the B-42 contention test is also run. The two tests cover different contenders despite sharing the lock primitive.
  - `B-004/X-I10` ↔ B-31, B-31.5: B-42’s no-rewrite hash behavior and B-31/B-31.5 checkpoint identity establish complementary idempotency layers: artifact-level content idempotency versus batch-level index resume identity.
  - `B-004/X-A01` ↔ B-25, B-27, B-36: B-27 air-gap disables all external network, but B-36 assumes a remote sidecar, B-42 schedules web research, and B-25 supports TCP Empire. The precedence/gating contract for those three integrations under `air_gapped=true` is unstated.
  - `B-004/X-A03` ↔ B-25, B-27: B-27 privacy redaction covers cloud model context, but whether B-42 FlashResearch queries/results and B-25 Empire event payloads pass the same redactor/audit trail is not stated.
  - `B-004/X-C02` ↔ B-25: B-25’s literal schema 101->102, B-42’s baseline-28/current+1 language, and orientation’s later schema values describe different repository snapshots. No numeric migration order can be synthesized safely from this chunk.
  - `B-005/X08` ↔ B-37, B-43: : B-43 uses B-37 to render hardware-appropriate runtime defaults, creates but does not populate B-42’s Cosmos directory, and reuses C-28’s structured toolchain/idempotency pattern (<code>F08:8-10, 116-124, 493-513</code>).
- **Superseded:** `SUP-056` from `B42-E17`: `cosmos_get(name) -> Result<Artifact>` blocks up to 30 seconds to refresh content older than 2× cadence; on timeout it returns stale artifact with WARN metadata.
- **Superseded:** `SUP-057` from `B42-E22`: Status is `FRESH` at age ≤ cadence, `STALE` at age ≤2× cadence, and `DECAYED` above 2×; DECAYED drops to SHADOW, warns callers, and moves to front of refresh queue.
- **Superseded:** `SUP-058` from `B42-E24`: New CosmosDrawer lists status/refreshed time and per-artifact manual refresh; MonitorWindow shows `cosmos: X fresh / Y stale / Z decayed` only during PLANNING/FRAMING; `get_cosmos_status` and `trigger_cosmos_refresh` become live Tauri commands.
- **Superseded:** `SUP-059` from `B42-E26`: Produced symbols: `CosmosWorker`, `cosmos_get`, `CosmosStatus::{FRESH,STALE,DECAYED}`, `CosmosStage`, `GroundingMethod::CosmosCheck`, `compile_cosmos_briefing`, four events, and 12 seed artifacts. Consumers are B-35 indexes, ResearchTree, SourceQualityTable, IndexLock, Layer-1A scheduler; reachable from hourly tick, three briefing buil…
- **Superseded:** `SUP-060` from `B42-E35`: Named tests cover cadence/idempotency, indexer contention, hybrid-scoring fairness, grounding failure rows, 1,500-token/ShortCoder fallback, and DECAYED warning/no-silent-stale behavior.
- **Superseded:** `SUP-061` from `B42-E38`: blake3 follows anti-stale rule. `min_source_tier` defaults T1 because consumers treat Cosmos as authoritative; T2/T3 belong in active research where contradictions can surface.
- **Superseded:** `SUP-062` from `B42-E39`: 1,500 tokens is chosen to leave ≥18K headroom in a 25K local ceiling. DECAYED content is retained because WARNed stale data is preferable to no data when offline.
- **Superseded:** `SUP-063` from `B42-C01`: `cosmos_get` calls age >2× “stale,” while the formal enum calls that `DECAYED`; formal `STALE` is age between one and two cadences. Lazy-refresh trigger is therefore DECAYED-only as written.
- **Superseded:** `SUP-064` from `B42-C02`: Spec reference says research writes SHADOW then becomes ACTIVE after FPR; task state model says FRESH/STALE/DECAYED, with only DECAYED mapping to SHADOW. How newly written SHADOW/FPR promotion composes with freshness is not specified.
- **Superseded:** `SUP-066` from `SD-B42-05`: Addition/conflict: source calls future Visual Auditor B-43, conflicting with current B-43 one-line-install naming; orientation correctly flags this drift. Source also distinguishes formal STALE from DECAYED even though `cosmos_get` loosely calls >2× stale (L153-L190, L404-L408).
- **Superseded:** `SUP-098` from `SUMMARY/SUM-0375`: Knowledge errors degrade locally: a failed grammar is isolated and recorded; an artifact error cannot block code indexing; a stale Cosmos fetch can warn and use stale data; B-4 embedding failure goes through a circuit-breaker/degradation path rather than panic (B-4, B-30, B-35, B-42).
- `B42-E01`: Add reusable cross-project knowledge for current design trends, app-store rules, UX patterns, IP boundaries, palettes, and SaaS monetization so similar product ideas reuse prior research rather than repeat $35-55/30-90-minute work. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:338)_
- `B42-E02`: B-35 supplies artifact indexing, B-23 supplies Layer-1A growth/decay scheduling, B-31 supplies coexistence locks; B-40 classifies project and B-41 audits against Cosmos. B-24/B-25/B-27/B-28/B-37/B-38 are parallel-safe. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:339)_
- `B42-E03`: Refresh reuses Layer-1A: scheduled web research -> blake3 artifact -> `SHADOW` -> `ACTIVE` only after FPR threshold. FlashResearch converges to storage, CatRAG/A-RAG gain a second user-global collection, and cadence is domain half-life based (nightly fast-moving, weekly slow in the reference rationale). _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:340)_
- `B42-E04`: Cosmos lives at `~/.elai/cosmos/`, user-scoped and outside every project repo. It uses B-35-style Markdown plus TOML metadata with blake3 hashes and separate `cosmos_global` vector collection. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:341)_
- `B42-E05`: Indexing reuses B-32 chunking and B-35 hybrid vector + BM25 reciprocal-rank fusion. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:342)_
- `B42-E06`: Each artifact declares `refresh_cadence: daily|weekly|monthly|quarterly` and a `research_query` template; Layer-1A ticks launch FlashResearch and convergence writes Cosmos. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:343)_
- `B42-E07`: Designer, B-40 idea-framer, and B-41 per-level auditor inject `AVAILABLE_COSMOS`, filtered by detected `ProjectClass` and tier relevance. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:344)_
- `B42-E08`: `GroundingMethod::CosmosCheck` is the eighth method after ArtifactCheck and handles project-class-relevant design, IP, monetization, app-store, and marketing claims. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:345)_
- `B42-E09`: Every refresh compares blake3 content; unchanged content produces no rewrite, saving disk and dashboard/SSB noise. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:346)_
- `B42-E10`: Artifacts older than 2× cadence become `SHADOW`; consumers are warned and refresh is reprioritized. B-31 locks allow code indexing and Cosmos scheduling to coexist cleanly. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:347)_
- `B42-E11`: Layout is `manifest.toml`, `artifacts/<name>.md` + `<name>.toml`, and `index/cosmos_global.bm25` + `cosmos_global.vec`; examples include design trends, iOS guidelines, UX patterns, and copyright/trade dress. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:348)_
- `B42-E12`: Manifest `schema_version=1`; each `[[cosmos.artifacts]]` has `name`, `project_classes`, `refresh_cadence`, `min_source_tier`, `research_query`, `max_size_bytes`, and a `tier_relevance` map. At least 12 entries ship. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:349)_
- `B42-E13`: `design_trends_2026`: ConsumerMobileApp/WebApp/SaaSDashboard, weekly, T1, 32,768 bytes, relevance concepts .9/phases .5/steps .2/babysteps 0. `ios_app_store_guidelines`: ConsumerMobileApp, monthly, T1/apple.com, 65,536 bytes, concepts .7/phases .4/babysteps .6/plan 1.0. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:350)_
- `B42-E14`: `CosmosWorker::tick()` runs hourly by default and skips artifacts not due. _(anchor: DEEP-B-004.discovery.md:351)_

The complete chain is inferred; each component a… _(anchor: 02-B-PLAN-SUMMARY.discovery.md:626)_

### B-registers: Cross-file IX, summary deltas, ambiguity/conflict, inferences, audits
- **Status:** cross-cutting registers (not a single plan unit)
- **Anchors:** B-AGGREGATE.md register sections
- **Composes with:** unit entries above; IX edges also summarized under per-unit **Composes with**
- **Interaction records (IX)** — 98:
  - `B-001/X-001`: B-10’s profile/overlap producer feeds B-6’s context visualization. The index’s shipped record confirms real snapshot/overlap plumbing, while B-10 retains unresolved proof for provider detection, five-site replacement, reduction emissions, and parity. (B0:L108, L152; B10:L44-88, L137-165)
  - `B-001/X-002`: A model-aware TaskRunner can allocate nonzero slots, filter SSB by agent-file relevance, emit both computed-budget and reduction evidence, and let V2 render a truthful chart. This chain is composed from explicit endpoints; whether every link shipped is not established by this chunk. (B10:L44-117; B0:L108)
  - `B-001/X-003`: B-11’s visual rules govern all B-10/B-20/B-38/B-41 panels: exact type/color/layout tokens and no decorative animation remain global even for newly added Gates, failure, context, and audit surfaces. (B11:L72-138)
  - `B-001/X-004`: B-20’s IPC end state depends strictly on B-18B. Until then, both in-process and IPC-adapter gates exist; dashboard submit reaches the former, and deletion is explicitly unsafe. (B20:L16-27, L78-94, L164-175)
  - `B-001/X-005`: B-20 success rows satisfy B-7 positive integration evidence, and B-20 classification feeds Auditor before raw output. B-41 then scopes the Auditor by tree level; these are complementary evidence granularity changes, not competing taxonomies. (B20:L122-152; B41:L1-10)
  - `B-001/X-006`: B-38 gates PLANNING -> TESTING_FIRST using existing B-8/B-17 grounding; B-41’s per-tier gates occur after decomposition outputs and feed B-22 auto-advance. Ambiguity readiness does not substitute for zero-BLOCK tier evidence. (B38:L132-177; B41:L150-154)
  - `B-001/X-007`: Intended planning chain is idea framing -> ambiguity/grounding readiness -> tier generation -> scoped audit/rescope -> per-level auto-advance -> execution. This order is synthesis; individual transitions are explicit, and B-41 navigator spawning remains deferred. (B38:L132-177; B41:L56-80, L123-154; B0:L177-180)
  - `B-001/X-008`: B-41 Concepts consumes B-40 `FramedIdea`; absence degrades to WARN/coarse rules. B-42 is planned grounding fuel for Concepts/Phases, yet its scheduler is deferred, so the audit must tolerate missing fresh Cosmos rather than invent it. (B41:L12-18, L286-287, L358-362; B0:L181-184)
  - `B-001/X-009`: B-22 evidence and B-41 verdict evidence are additive: five level-specific checks extend rather than replace the six autonomous checks. High-level autonomous mode can remain wire-dark in CLI even while the dashboard toggle and stats work. (B41:L150-154; B0:L255-284)
  - `B-001/X-010`: B-39 text-only answer substitution bypasses tools, so B-20 dispatch/gating and B-41 implementation auditing do not run on that path. B-39E is the deferred bridge back into the normal tool loop. (B0:L343-370; B20:L164-170)
  - `B-001/X-011`: Remote sidecar startup and B-36 health/settings/search commands form a complete remote-control UX: SSH/tunnel/backoff/overlay precede live sidecar commands; cancellation and health-shape handling are explicit, but request cancellation and D.5 child termination still have defects. (B0:L114-119, L202-225)
  - `B-001/X-012`: Determinism spans sources: injected/seeded model/profile behavior, overlap sorting, exact visual tokens/timings, fixed rule order, score temperature 0.1, two-snapshot streak, and deterministic tests. Each is a separate invariant and none authorizes random UI/audit/scoring order. (B10:L69-88; B11:L72-138; B38:L45-49; B41:L352-354)
  - `B-002/X-001`: B-1 is the explicit prerequisite for both B-4 model/vector placement and B-31 checkpoint metadata placement; it also declares B-2 parallel-safe (B-1 lines 6-8; B-4 lines 5-8; B-31 lines 16-19).
  - `B-002/X-002`: B-1’s canonical helper surface is the shared authority for CLI/orchestrator/dashboard reachability; B-4 and B-31 must consume the same <code>.elai_cc</code> root rather than introducing feature-local literals (B-1 lines 58-69, 110-127).
  - `B-002/X-003`: B-2’s V2 StatusBar/Monitor consumers live inside the single Workspace shell that B-12 declares canonical; the phase writer is orchestrator-side and therefore independent of shell reorganization (B-2 lines 10-44; B-12 lines 11-24, 161-164).
  - `B-002/X-004`: B-2, B-4, B-24, B-31, B-34, and B-39 all define SSB observability, but with different control semantics: B-2 writes are best-effort; B-24 Tier 1 can veto; B-31 events evidence lifecycle; B-34 logs discovery; B-39 failures fall through (their respective wiring/failure sections).
  - `B-002/X-005`: B-4’s model staging explicitly relies on B-1’s <code>models_dir_path</code>; B-1 independently bans old <code>.elai/models/</code> literals (B-1 lines 87-89; B-4 lines 80-89).
  - `B-002/X-006`: B-31’s idempotent <code>migrateAbsolutePathKeys</code> is explicitly a post-B-1 compatibility migration, tying resume identity to relative canonical paths (B-31 lines 161-165).
  - `B-002/X-007`: B-31 and B-34 are declared parallel-safe, but both name B-29: B-31 supplies resilient/incremental index lifecycle while B-34 consumes B-29’s symbol cache/payload contract (B-31 lines 17-19, 449-451; B-34 lines 9-11).
  - `B-002/X-008`: B-31 explicitly enables B-35 artifact indexing to coexist with code indexing through shared lock primitives (B-31 lines 462-465).
  - `B-002/X-009`: B-12 depends on B-3 registration, B-7 decomposition, and B-11 tokens; B-24 depends on B-7 media-bearing PipelineSpec and B-22 autonomous iteration; none of those prerequisites is silently supplied by this chunk (B-12 lines 7-9; B-24 lines 8-10).
  - `B-002/X-010`: B-39 depends on B-5 role configuration, B-8 PipelineGrc production wiring, and B-14 streaming; its passthrough fallback deliberately preserves B-14 behavior when disabled/skipped/failing (B-39 lines 8-12, 245-278).
  - `B-002/X-011`: Once B-1 and B-31 compose, checkpoint/file-hash/lock state and the DKG/BM25/vector data they protect resolve beneath one canonical project root; neither source authorizes a second metadata root.
  - `B-002/X-012`: B-2’s phase events become visible only after both producer wiring and a live V2 read path; B-12 establishes where the StatusBar lives, while B-36 (outside this chunk) remains the named backend bridge.
  - `B-002/X-013`: B-31’s crash-safe batching protects the indexes queried by B-4; therefore duplicate-free resume is upstream data-integrity evidence for query traces/recall, even though neither file defines a direct B-31→B-4 API.
  - `B-002/X-014`: B-34 is read-only over graph/cache payloads; B-31’s Index/Watch locks govern mutation. A coherent implementation can let entry-point reads continue against the last completed snapshot during contention, but this behavior is not promised by either source.
  - `B-002/X-015`: V2 commands are the common bridge for B-2 state, B-4 knowledge, B-24 media, B-31 cancellation/progress, and B-34 entry-point display; exact command registration is repeatedly delegated to B-36 rather than owned by B-12’s shell.
