  - `SUMMARY/SUM-0303`: Extend B-17 GroundingSweepGate with `ambiguity = 1 - sum(clarity_i * weight_i)` and an overall pass threshold <=0.2.
  - `SUMMARY/SUM-0304`: Greenfield weights: Goal 40%, Constraint 30%, Success Criteria 30%. Brownfield: Goal 35%, Constraint 25%, Success 25%, Context 15%.
  - `SUMMARY/SUM-0305`: Per-dimension floors: Goal >=0.75, Constraint >=0.65, Success >=0.70, brownfield Context >=0.60. A low dimension blocks even if the aggregate passes.
  - `SUMMARY/SUM-0306`: Require two consecutive ready snapshots; one noisy snapshot cannot pass. Store the streak in `SessionState` and reset it on plan revision.
  - `SUMMARY/SUM-0307`: LLM scoring temperature is 0.1.
  - `SUMMARY/SUM-0308`: Token allocation starts at 2,048 and doubles on truncation for at most five retries; format errors also have at most five retries.
  - `SUMMARY/SUM-0309`: Events: `ambiguity_scored`, `ambiguity_floor_failed`, `ambiguity_streak_advanced`.
  - `SUMMARY/SUM-0310`: Verdict ordering: any ungrounded claim -> BLOCK; else ambiguity >0.2 or any floor failure -> BLOCK; else streak below two -> WAIT; otherwise ALLOW.

  - `B-003/I-005.`: B-15's single remaining direct-time leak is in `task_runner.rs`, the same broad hot-path owner used by B-8 and B-40. New wiring there should use `ClockProvider` to avoid re-growing the quarantined debt; the exact affected function cannot be identified without opening code. (`E-B15-014`, `E-B15-015`, `E-B8-018`, `E-B40-016`)
  - `B005-I-001`: : composing B-6, B-17, B-22, and B-26 yields a control loop of producer verdict → non-vacuity regression proof → autonomous decision/human escalation → live Monitor/intervention notification. Each edge is explicit, but this end-to-end sequence is not stated in one canonical file.
  - `B005-I-002`: : because B-22 can auto-advance only after evidence and B-17 fails CI when gate inputs go vacuous, B-17 is indirectly a safety gate for autonomous execution, not merely dashboard coverage.
  - `B005-I-003`: : B-26’s real-invoke-or-mock architecture allows UI acceptance work to proceed before B-36, but production evidence still needs the real Tauri route; mock success cannot prove producer reachability.
  - `B005-I-004`: : B-28’s “preferred mode” should be treated as a hint constrained by deterministic capability/safety rules, because allowing it to override unsupported FIM or new-file STRUCTURED fallback would violate explicit bans.
  - `B005-I-005`: : B-29’s reverse index is file-based even when a symbol is queried, so an implementation must first resolve the symbol to one or more seed files before its documented BFS; that missing bridge is implementation work, not permission to scan the whole graph.
  - `B005-I-006`: : B-29’s PostToolUse placement means <code>IMPACT_SUMMARY</code> grounds audit of an edit that already occurred; it cannot be a pre-write prevention gate unless an additional pre-tool hook is added.
  - `B005-I-007`: : B-32’s 2,000 “character” invariant is operationally a maximum of 2,000 UTF-8 bytes, because all canonical arithmetic and IDs use byte length/offset.
  - `B005-I-008`: : B-32’s default cap can lose the tail of a giant AST/line chunk; retrieval completeness for minified input is preserved only because detected minified files go through repeated character chunks rather than truncating one AST region.
  - `B005-I-009`: : B-43’s no-auto-boot rule and required post-install doctor exit zero imply <code>check_sidecar_health</code> must distinguish “installed but intentionally stopped” from an installation failure, likely as non-Fail, though the source does not state the diagnostic level.
  - `B005-I-010`: : B-43’s eager model staging and B-28’s FIM capability detection are complementary: a local tier can be ready for fast FIM after install, but only if runtime model selection resolves the 3B/7B command ambiguity.
  - `B005-I-011`: : B-43’s unsupported-architecture hard error is a product-install gate, not just a local-sidecar gate; therefore even cloud-capable Apple/Linux ARM users cannot use this one-line flow until E-51 or a later installer changes the locked decision.
  - `B005-I-012`: : B-43’s partial-state, hash, and preservation rules make reruns idempotent only for validated artifacts; corrupt venvs/config conflicts intentionally stop for user action rather than self-heal destructively.
